### S004 — Abstract heading
**Source:** p.1 S004
**Original:** Abstract  
**中文：** 摘要

<span id="S005"></span>
### S005 — Abstract
**Source:** p.1 S005
**Original:** Diffusion generative models unlock new possibilities for inverse problems as they allow for the incorporation of strong empirical priors in scientific inference. Recently, diffusion models are repurposed for solving inverse problems using Gaussian approximations to conditional densities of the reverse process via Tweedie’s formula to parameterise the mean, complemented with various heuristics. To address various challenges arising from these approximations, we leverage higher order information using Tweedie’s formula and obtain a statistically principled approximation. We further provide a theoretical guarantee specifically for posterior sampling which can lead to a better theoretical understanding of diffusion-based conditional sampling. Finally, we illustrate the empirical effectiveness of our approach for general linear inverse problems on toy synthetic examples as well as image restoration. We show that our method (i) removes any time-dependent step-size hyperparameters required by earlier methods, (ii) brings stability and better sample quality across multiple noise levels, (iii) is the only method that works in a stable way with variance exploding (VE) forward processes as opposed to earlier works.  
**中文：** 扩散生成模型为逆问题带来了新的可能，因为它们能够在科学推断中引入有力的经验先验。近期的研究将扩散模型用于求解逆问题：通过 Tweedie 公式参数化反向过程条件密度的均值，并以高斯分布近似该条件密度，同时辅以多种启发式设计。为应对这些近似带来的若干问题，我们利用 Tweedie 公式中的高阶信息，构造具有统计学原理依据的近似。我们还针对后验采样给出一项理论保证，这有助于加深对基于扩散模型的条件采样的理论理解。最后，我们通过一般线性逆问题中的合成玩具示例以及图像复原实验，展示所提方法的实证效果。结果表明，该方法：（i）不再需要早期方法所需的、随时间变化的步长超参数；（ii）在多个噪声水平下提高了稳定性和样本质量；（iii）与早期工作不同，是唯一能够在方差爆炸（variance exploding，VE）型前向过程中稳定运行的方法。

<span id="S006"></span>
### S006 — Section heading
**Source:** p.1 S006
**Original:** 1 Introduction  
**中文：** 1 引言

<span id="S007"></span>
### S007 — Introduction, opening paragraph (pp.1–2)
**Source:** pp.1–2 S007
**Original:** Due to the ease of scalability, diffusion models (Song et al., 2020; Ho et al., 2020) have received increased attention, producing a variety of improvements in the conditional generation setting such as adding classifier guidance (Dhariwal & Nichol, 2021), classifier free guidance (Ho & Salimans, 2022), conditional diffusion models (Batzolis et al., 2021; Karras et al., 2022, 2024) and DEFT (Doob’s h-transform Efficient FineTuning) (Denker et al., 2024). These methods are useful for problems where paired (\mathbf{x}_0,\mathbf{y}) data are available, see, e.g., Saharia et al. (2022); Abramson et al. (2024). However, paired data is not always available, and there are cases where we would like to study alternatives to classical Bayesian approaches to solve inverse problems (Tarantola, 2005; Stuart, 2010) to build samplers for complicated conditional distributions with a known observation operator mapping \mathbf{x}_0 to the observed data \mathbf{y} that is used to express a known likelihood p(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x}_0). With their flexibility, diffusion models replace handcrafted priors on the latent signal with pretrained and strong empirical priors. For example, given a latent signal \mathbf{x}_0 (say a face image), we can train a diffusion model to sample from the prior p(\mathbf{x}_0). The idea for solving inverse problems is to leverage the extraordinary modelling power of diffusion models to learn samplers for priors and couple this with a given likelihood p(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x}_0) for a given data y to sample from the posterior p(\mathbf{x}_0|\mathbf{y}) for inverse problems. However, designing a diffusion model for the posterior comes with challenges due to intractability. Despite its challenges, this approach has been recently taking off with lots of activity in the field, e.g. for compressed sensing (Bora et al., 2017; Kadkhodaie & Simoncelli, 2021), projecting score-based stochastic differential equations (SDEs) (Song et al., 2021b), gradient-based approaches (Daras et al., 2022; Chung et al., 2022b), magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) by approximating annealed Langevin dynamics with approximate scores (Jalal et al., 2021), image restoration (Kawar et al., 2022), score-based models as priors but with a normalizing flow approach (Feng et al., 2023), variational approaches (Mardani et al., 2023; Feng & Bouman, 2023). Most relevant ideas to us, which we will review in detail in Section 5, use Tweedie’s formula (Efron, 2011; Laumont et al., 2022) to approximate the smoothed likelihood, e.g. Diffusion posterior sampling (DPS) (Chung et al., 2022a; 2023) and pseudo-guided diffusion models (ΠGDM) (Song et al., 2023). Similar approaches are also exploited using singular-value decomposition (SVD) based approaches (Kawar et al., 2021). In this work, we develop an approach which builds a tighter approximation to optimal formulae for approximating the scores of the posterior diffusion model.  
**中文：** 扩散模型易于扩展，因此受到越来越多的关注，并推动了条件生成领域的多种改进，例如加入分类器引导（classifier guidance）（Dhariwal & Nichol, 2021）、无分类器引导（classifier-free guidance）（Ho & Salimans, 2022）、条件扩散模型（Batzolis et al., 2021; Karras et al., 2022, 2024），以及 DEFT（Doob 的 h-变换高效微调，Doob’s h-transform Efficient FineTuning）（Denker et al., 2024）。当存在成对的 (x0,y) 数据时，这些方法很有用，参见例如 Saharia et al. (2022) 和 Abramson et al. (2024)。然而，成对数据并非总是可得；在某些情况下，我们希望研究经典贝叶斯方法之外的逆问题求解方案（Tarantola, 2005; Stuart, 2010），以便在已知观测算子将 x0 映射到观测数据 y、且该算子可用于表示已知似然 p(y|x0) 的条件下，为复杂条件分布构造采样器。凭借灵活性，扩散模型以预训练得到的强经验先验取代了手工设计的潜在信号先验。例如，给定潜在信号 x0（比如一张人脸图像），我们可以训练扩散模型从先验 p(x0) 中采样。求解逆问题的思路是，利用扩散模型强大的建模能力来学习先验分布的采样器，再将其与给定似然 p(y|x0) 结合；对于给定数据 y，由此从逆问题的后验 p(x0|y) 中采样。然而，由于存在不可解析性，为后验直接设计扩散模型面临挑战。尽管如此，该方向近期发展迅速，相关研究包括压缩感知（Bora et al., 2017; Kadkhodaie & Simoncelli, 2021）、投影 score-based 随机微分方程（SDE）（Song et al., 2021b）、基于梯度的方法（Daras et al., 2022; Chung et al., 2022b）、通过近似 score 对退火 Langevin 动力学进行近似的磁共振成像（MRI）（Jalal et al., 2021）、图像复原（Kawar et al., 2022）、将 score-based 模型用作先验并结合正规化流的方法（Feng et al., 2023），以及变分方法（Mardani et al., 2023; Feng & Bouman, 2023）。与本文最相关、并将在第5节详细回顾的思路，使用 Tweedie 公式（Efron, 2011; Laumont et al., 2022）近似平滑后的似然，例如扩散后验采样（diffusion posterior sampling，DPS）（Chung et al., 2022a; 2023）和伪引导扩散模型（ΠGDM）（Song et al., 2023）。基于奇异值分解（SVD）的方法也采用了类似思路（Kawar et al., 2021）。本文提出一种方法，以更紧的近似来逼近后验扩散模型 score 的最优表达式。

<span id="S009"></span>
### S009 — Problem setup and contribution
**Source:** p.2 S009
**Original:** This paper is devoted to developing novel methods to solving inverse problems, given a latent (target) signal \mathbf{x}_0 \in \mathbb{R}^{d_x}, noisy observed data \mathbf{y} \in \mathbb{R}^{d_y}, a known linear observation map \mathbf{H}, and a pretrained diffusion prior. The main tool we use is Tweedie’s formula to obtain both the mean and the covariance for approximating diffused likelihoods, to be used for building the final posterior score approximation. This is as opposed to previous works which only utilised first moment approximations using Tweedie’s formula (Chung et al., 2022a; Song et al., 2023). We show that utilising covariance approximation, with moment projections, provides a principled scheme with improved performance, which we term Tweedie Moment Projected Diffusions (TMPD).  
**中文：** 本文旨在为如下设定开发新的逆问题求解方法：给定潜在（目标）信号 \(\mathbf{x}_0\in\mathbb{R}^{d_x}\)、含噪观测数据 \(\mathbf{y}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_y}\)、已知线性观测映射 \(\mathbf{H}\)，以及预训练扩散先验。本文使用的核心工具是 Tweedie 公式，以同时获得均值和协方差来近似扩散后的似然，并據此构造最终的后验 score 近似。此前的工作只利用 Tweedie 公式进行一阶矩近似（Chung et al., 2022a; Song et al., 2023）；与之不同，本文表明，将协方差近似与矩投影结合，可以得到一个有原理依据且性能更好的方案，并将其称为 Tweedie Moment Projected Diffusions（TMPD，Tweedie 矩投影扩散）。

<span id="S010"></span>
### S010 — Figure 1 discussion
**Source:** p.2 S010
**Original:** To demonstrate our method briefly, Figure 1 demonstrates a sampling scenario of a Gaussian random field (GRF) whose mean and variance entries are plotted under ‘Analytic’ column1. We demonstrate the approximations under this setting provided by our method (TMPD) and its diagonal (cheaper) version (DTMPD), compared with ΠGDM (Song et al., 2023), and DPS (Chung et al., 2022a). The figure demonstrates the optimality of our method: Our first and second moment approximations become exact in this case. This results in a drastic performance improvement stemming from the statistical optimality of our method for near-Gaussian settings and also unlocks a possible line for theoretical research for understanding similar diffusion models for inverse problems.  
**中文：** 为简要展示所提方法，图1给出了高斯随机场（Gaussian random field，GRF）下的一个采样情形；“Analytic”（解析）列绘出了用于误差计算的1500个样本的经验均值和方差。图中展示了所提 TMPD 及其对角近似（计算代价较低）版本 DTMPD 在该设置下的结果，并与 ΠGDM（Song et al., 2023）和 DPS（Chung et al., 2022a）进行比较。图中体现出所提方法在该情形下的最优性：其一阶和二阶矩近似均变为精确结果。对于近高斯设置，这种统计最优性带来了显著的性能提升，也为理解同类逆问题扩散模型提供了一个潜在的理论研究方向。

<span id="S011"></span>
### S011 — Article roadmap
**Source:** p.2 S011
**Original:** In what follows, we will first introduce the technical background in Section 2 and then describe TMPD in detail in Section 3. We will then provide some theoretical results about our method in Section 4 and provide a discussion to closely related work in literature in Section 5. Finally, Section 6 will present experiments on Gaussian mixtures, image inpainting and super-resolution, demonstrating quantitative and qualitative improvements provided by TMPD.  
**中文：** 下文首先在第2节介绍技术背景，随后在第3节详细描述 TMPD。第4节给出该方法的一些理论结果，第5节讨论密切相关的已有工作。最后，第6节将报告高斯混合、图像修补和超分辨率实验，展示 TMPD 带来的定量与定性改进。

<span id="S013"></span>
### S013 — heading
**Source:** p.3 S013
**Original:** 2 Technical Background  
**中文：** 2 技术背景

<span id="S014"></span>
### S014 — paragraph
**Source:** p.3 S014
**Original:** In score based generative models (SGMs) (Song et al., 2020) and denoising diffusion probabilistic models (DDPM) (Ho et al., 2020), the goal is to sample from a target distribution p0 := pdata. To that end, an artificial path pt is introduced, with the property that pt will approach N(0,I) for large t, i.e., pt → N(0,Id) as t → ∞. Then, one learns to reverse this process, in order to transform samples from a standard normal distribution into samples from pdata.  
**中文：** 在基于 score 的生成模型（score-based generative models，SGM）（Song et al., 2020）和去噪扩散概率模型（denoising diffusion probabilistic models，DDPM）（Ho et al., 2020）中，目标是从目标分布 p₀ := p_data 中采样。为此，引入一条人工构造的概率路径 p_t；当 t 足够大时，p_t 将趋近于标准高斯分布，即 p_t→𝒩(0,I_d)，t→∞。随后学习这一过程的逆过程，从而把标准正态分布中的样本变换为来自 p_data 的样本。

<span id="S015"></span>
### S015 — paragraph
**Source:** p.3 S015
**Original:** More recent advances in generative modelling include methods with artificial paths based on stochastic interpolants or continuous normalizing flows, see, e.g., Albergo & Vanden-Eijnden (2022); Lipman et al. (2022). Conditional flow matching draws inspiration from the denoising score matching approach, but generalizes to matching vector fields directly. We have focused our approach to denoising-diffusion models in the SGM paradigm because of readily available pretrained diffusion models.  
**中文：** 生成建模领域较新的进展还包括基于随机插值（stochastic interpolants）或连续正规化流（continuous normalizing flows）构造人工路径的方法，参见例如 Albergo & Vanden-Eijnden (2022) 和 Lipman et al. (2022)。条件流匹配（conditional flow matching）受到去噪 score 匹配方法的启发，但将匹配对象直接推广为向量场。由于已有预训练扩散模型可供使用，本文将方法重点放在 SGM 范式下的去噪扩散模型上。

<span id="S016"></span>
### S016 — paragraph
**Source:** p.3 S016
**Original:** In the SGM paradigm, a stochastic differential equation (SDE) is used to noise the data, and the interpolation parameter t will take continuous values in t ∈ [0,T]. In the DDPM setting, t is discrete. However, the DDPM Markov chain can be seen as a discretization of the SDE (Song et al., 2020).  
**中文：** 在 SGM 范式中，使用随机微分方程（stochastic differential equation，SDE）对数据加噪，插值参数 t 连续取值于区间 [0,T]。在 DDPM 中，t 是离散的。不过，DDPM 马尔可夫链可以看作该 SDE 的离散化（Song et al., 2020）。

<span id="S017"></span>
### S017 — paragraph
**Source:** p.3 S017
**Original:** Recent developments have improved the noising schedule of score-based diffusion models for image data that parameterise the transition kernels of the forward process in terms of the signal-to-noise ratio \(\sigma_t^2\), \(p_t(\mathbf{x}_t|\mathbf{x}_0)=\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{x}_t;s_t\mathbf{x}_0,s_t^2\sigma_t^2\mathbf{I}_{d_x})\) (Karras et al., 2022; 2024). In this paper, we focus derivations on the Variance Preserving (VP) SDE formulation, although our approach can be generalised and derived for other SDEs, such as the Variance Exploding (VE) SDE (see Appendix C). The VP transition kernel that we choose to focus our derivation is given by setting  
**中文：** 近期工作改进了面向图像数据的 score-based 扩散模型加噪日程，并用信噪比 σ_t² 参数化前向过程的转移核：p_t(x_t|x_0)=𝒩(x_t; s_t x_0, s_t²σ_t²I_{d_x})（Karras et al., 2022; 2024）。本文的推导重点采用方差保持（variance preserving，VP）SDE；不过，该方法也可以推广并针对其他 SDE 推导，例如方差爆炸（variance exploding，VE）SDE（见附录 C）。本文选用的 VP 转移核通过如下设定给出：

<span id="S018"></span>
### S018 — equation
**Source:** p.3 S018
**Original:** \(p_t(\mathbf{x}_t|\mathbf{x}_0)=\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{x}_t;\sqrt{\alpha_t}\mathbf{x}_0,v_t\mathbf{I}_{d_x})\), where \(\alpha_t:=\exp(-\int_0^t\beta(s)\,ds)\) and \(v_t:=1-\alpha_t\).  
**中文：** 公式保留原变量、参数和运算：\[p_t(\mathbf{x}_t|\mathbf{x}_0)=\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{x}_t;\sqrt{\alpha_t}\mathbf{x}_0,v_t\mathbf{I}_{d_x}),\qquad \alpha_t:=\exp\!\left(-\int_0^t\beta(s)\,ds\right),\qquad v_t:=1-\alpha_t.\]

<span id="S019"></span>
### S019 — equation
**Source:** p.3 S019
**Original:** \(\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}_t=-\tfrac12\beta(t)\mathbf{x}_t\,\mathrm{d}t+\sqrt{\beta(t)}\,\mathrm{d}\mathbf{w}_t,\quad \mathbf{x}_0\sim p_0=p_{\mathrm{data}}.\) (1)  
**中文：** 公式（1）保留原符号、系数和编号：\[\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}_t=-\tfrac12\beta(t)\mathbf{x}_t\,\mathrm{d}t+\sqrt{\beta(t)}\,\mathrm{d}\mathbf{w}_t,\qquad \mathbf{x}_0\sim p_0=p_{\mathrm{data}}.\]

<span id="S020"></span>
### S020 — equation
**Source:** p.3 S020
**Original:** \(\mathrm{d}\mathbf{z}_t=\tfrac12\beta(T-t)\mathbf{z}_t\,\mathrm{d}t+\beta(T-t)\nabla_{\mathbf{z}_t}\log p_{T-t}(\mathbf{z}_t)\,\mathrm{d}t+\sqrt{\beta(T-t)}\,\mathrm{d}\bar{\mathbf{w}}_t,\quad \mathbf{z}_0\sim p_T.\)  
**中文：** 原文给出的反向 SDE（本页显示式未见独立编号）按原式保留：\[\mathrm{d}\mathbf{z}_t=\tfrac12\beta(T-t)\mathbf{z}_t\,\mathrm{d}t+\beta(T-t)\nabla_{\mathbf{z}_t}\log p_{T-t}(\mathbf{z}_t)\,\mathrm{d}t+\sqrt{\beta(T-t)}\,\mathrm{d}\bar{\mathbf{w}}_t,\qquad \mathbf{z}_0\sim p_T.\]

<span id="S021"></span>
### S021 — paragraph
**Source:** p.3 S021
**Original:** A parameterisation that performs well in practice is β(t)=β_min+t(β_max−β_min). In the diffusion modelling literature, the time-rescaled OU process is also sometimes called a Variance Preserving SDE. This is not the only SDE that is suitable for the forward process. See Appendix C for details on a time-rescaled Brownian motion (Variance Exploding SDE (Song et al., 2020)).  
**中文：** 一种实践效果较好的参数化形式为 β(t)=β_min+t(β_max−β_min)。在扩散模型文献中，经过时间重参数化的 Ornstein–Uhlenbeck（OU）过程有时也称为方差保持 SDE。它并非唯一适用于前向过程的 SDE；关于时间重参数化布朗运动（方差爆炸 SDE），详见附录 C（Song et al., 2020）。

<span id="S022"></span>
### S022 — paragraph
**Source:** p.3 S022
**Original:** There are two usual approximations to solve the SDE in Equation (2). First, we do not know \(p_T\), since it is a noised version of the distribution \(p_{\mathrm{data}}\). However for \(T\) large enough, we can approximate \(p_T\approx p_{\mathrm{ref}}=\mathcal{N}(0,\mathbf{I}_{d_x})\). We also do not have \(\nabla\log p_{T-t}\) which we need for the drift in Equation (2). This can be circumvented by approximating the drift using score-matching techniques (Hyvärinen, 2005; Ho et al., 2020). These methods construct an estimate of the score function by solving the score matching problem in the form of \(s_\theta(\mathbf{x}_t,t)\approx\nabla_{\mathbf{x}_t}\log p_t(\mathbf{x}_t)\). This score can also be used in the setting of DDPM (Ho et al., 2020).  
**中文：** 通常通过两项近似来求解式（2）中的 SDE。第一，p_T 未知，因为它是分布 p_data 的加噪版本；但当 T 足够大时，可以用 p_ref≈𝒩(0,I_{d_x}) 近似 p_T。第二，漂移项需要用到的 ∇log p_{T−t} 也未知。可以采用 score 匹配技术（Hyvärinen, 2005; Ho et al., 2020）近似该漂移项。这些方法通过求解 score 匹配问题，构造 score 函数的估计 s_θ(x_t,t)≈∇_{x_t}log p_t(x_t)。该 score 也可用于 DDPM（Ho et al., 2020）。

<span id="S023"></span>
### S023 — heading
**Source:** p.3 S023
**Original:** 2.1 Conditional sampling for the linear inverse problem  
**中文：** 2.1 线性逆问题的条件采样

<span id="S024"></span>
### S024 — paragraph
**Source:** p.3 S024
**Original:** In the preceding section we introduced diffusion models as a method to sample from a target distribution \(p_{\mathrm{data}}\). We now suppose that we have access to measurements, or observations \(\mathbf{y}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_y}\) of \(\mathbf{x}_0\in\mathbb{R}^{d_x}\):  
**中文：** 上一节介绍了将扩散模型作为从目标分布 \(p_{\mathrm{data}}\) 中采样的方法。现在假设可以获得关于 \(\mathbf{x}_0\) 的测量值或观测值 \(\mathbf{y}\)，其中 \(\mathbf{y}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_y}\)、\(\mathbf{x}_0\in\mathbb{R}^{d_x}\)：

<span id="S025"></span>
### S025 — equation
**Source:** p.3 S025
**Original:** \(\mathbf{y}=\mathbf{H}\mathbf{x}_0+\mathbf{u},\quad \mathbf{u}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma_y^2\mathbf{I}_{d_y}).\) (2)  
**中文：** 测量模型式（2）原样保留变量、噪声方差和编号：\[\mathbf{y}=\mathbf{H}\mathbf{x}_0+\mathbf{u},\qquad \mathbf{u}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma_y^2\mathbf{I}_{d_y}).\]

<span id="S026"></span>
### S026 — paragraph
**Source:** p.3 S026
**Original:** We would then be interested in sampling from the conditional distribution of \(\mathbf{x}_0\) given \(\mathbf{y}\), i.e., \(p_{\mathrm{data}}(\cdot|\mathbf{y})\). To that end, we have to modify the reverse SDE. We would like to sample from the reverse SDE targeting \(p_{\mathrm{data}}(\cdot|\mathbf{y})\), instead of the one targeting \(p_{\mathrm{data}}\).  
**中文：** 此时，我们希望从给定 \(\mathbf{y}\) 条件下 \(\mathbf{x}_0\) 的条件分布 \(p_{\mathrm{data}}(\cdot|\mathbf{y})\) 中采样。为此，需要修改反向 SDE，使其以 \(p_{\mathrm{data}}(\cdot|\mathbf{y})\) 为目标，而不是以 \(p_{\mathrm{data}}\) 为目标。

<span id="S027"></span>
### S027 — paragraph
**Source:** p.4 S027
**Original:** Optimally, we would want to replace the score \(\nabla_{\mathbf{z}_t}\log p_{T-t}(\mathbf{z}_t)\) in Equation (2) with the posterior score \(\nabla_{\mathbf{z}_t}\log p_{T-t|y}(\mathbf{z}_t|\mathbf{y})\). Written in terms of the forward process, this coincides with  
**中文：** 理想情况下，我们希望将式（2）中的 score \(\nabla_{\mathbf{z}_t}\log p_{T-t}(\mathbf{z}_t)\) 替换为后验 score \(\nabla_{\mathbf{z}_t}\log p_{T-t|y}(\mathbf{z}_t|\mathbf{y})\)。用前向过程表示时，这等价于：

<span id="S028"></span>
### S028 — equation
**Source:** p.4 S028
**Original:** \(\nabla_{\mathbf{x}_t}\log p_{t|y}(\mathbf{x}_t|\mathbf{y})=\nabla_{\mathbf{x}_t}\log p_t(\mathbf{x}_t)+\nabla_{\mathbf{x}_t}\log p_{y|t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x}_t).\) (3)  
**中文：** 式（3）保留原条件方向、变量和编号：\[\nabla_{\mathbf{x}_t}\log p_{t|y}(\mathbf{x}_t|\mathbf{y})=\nabla_{\mathbf{x}_t}\log p_t(\mathbf{x}_t)+\nabla_{\mathbf{x}_t}\log p_{y|t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x}_t).\]

<span id="S029"></span>
### S029 — equation
**Source:** p.4 S029
**Original:** \(p_{y|t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x}_t)=\int p_{y|0}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x}_0)p_{0|t}(\mathbf{x}_0|\mathbf{x}_t)\,d\mathbf{x}_0.\) (4)  
**中文：** 式（4）保留原被积项、条件变量和编号：\[p_{y|t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x}_t)=\int p_{y|0}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x}_0)p_{0|t}(\mathbf{x}_0|\mathbf{x}_t)\,\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}_0.\]

<span id="S030"></span>
### S030 — paragraph
**Source:** p.4 S030
**Original:** which involves a marginalization over x0. The above integral is difficult to evaluate since the term p0|t(x0|xt) is only defined implicitly through running the diffusion model. One way around this is to train a neural network to directly approximate ∇log pt|y(xt|y) (Batzolis et al., 2021; Karras et al., 2022). Alternatively, if one already has access to an approximation of ∇xt log pt(xt), one can train an auxiliary network to approximate the term ∇log py|t(y|xt) in Equation (3), (Dhariwal & Nichol, 2021; Denker et al., 2024). However, these methods can be time and training-data intensive, as it is necessary to retrain networks for each conditional task as well have access to paired training data from the joint distribution of (x0,y). Alternatively, one could try to do a Monte-Carlo approximation of the score corresponding to Equation (4). But this needs evaluating the probability flow ODE together with its derivative (Song et al., 2020, Section D.2) for each sample, which is prohibitive, also suffers from high variance (Mohamed et al., 2020, Section 3).  
**中文：** 式（4）需要对 x₀ 进行边缘化。由于 p_{0|t}(x₀|x_t) 只能通过运行扩散模型隐式定义，上述积分难以直接计算。一种解决途径是训练神经网络，直接近似 ∇log p_{t|y}(x_t|y)（Batzolis et al., 2021; Karras et al., 2022）。另一种途径是在已有 ∇_{x_t}log p_t(x_t) 近似的情况下，训练辅助网络来近似式（3）中的 ∇log p_{y|t}(y|x_t)（Dhariwal & Nichol, 2021; Denker et al., 2024）。然而，这些方法耗时且需要大量训练数据，因为每个条件任务都必须重新训练网络，并且还需要来自联合分布 (x₀,y) 的成对训练数据。另一种思路是对式（4）对应的 score 作蒙特卡洛近似，但这要求针对每个样本计算概率流常微分方程（ODE）及其导数（Song et al., 2020，第 D.2 节），计算代价过高，而且方差也很大（Mohamed et al., 2020，第3节）。

<span id="S031"></span>
### S031 — heading
**Source:** p.4 S031
**Original:** 3 Tweedie Moment Projected Diffusions  
**中文：** 3 Tweedie 矩投影扩散

<span id="S032"></span>
### S032 — paragraph
**Source:** p.4 S032
**Original:** In this section, we first introduce Tweedie moment projections in Section 3.1 below. Our method relies on the approximation \(p_{0|t}(\mathbf{x}_0|\mathbf{x}_t)\approx\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{x}_0;\mathbf{m}_{0|t}(\mathbf{x}_t),\mathbf{C}_{0|t}(\mathbf{x}_t))\) to make the sampling process tractable. In that case, since the conditional distribution of y given x0 is also Gaussian, we can compute the integral in Equation (4) analytically—p_{y|t}(y|x_t) will just be another Gaussian in that case, its mean and covariance being determined through m_{0|t}, C_{0|t}, H and σ_y. In particular, we can then use this Gaussian to approximate ∇log p_{y|t}(y|x_t), since the score of a Gaussian is available in closed form.  
**中文：** 本节先在下文第3.1节介绍 Tweedie 矩投影。为使采样过程可处理，方法采用近似 \(p_{0|t}(\mathbf{x}_0|\mathbf{x}_t)\approx\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{x}_0;\mathbf{m}_{0|t}(\mathbf{x}_t),\mathbf{C}_{0|t}(\mathbf{x}_t))\)。在此近似下，由于给定 x₀ 时 y 的条件分布也是高斯分布，式（4）的积分可以解析计算；此时 p_{y|t}(y|x_t) 仍为高斯分布，其均值和协方差由 m_{0|t}、C_{0|t}、H 和 σ_y 决定。特别地，由于高斯分布的 score 有闭式表达，可以用该高斯分布近似 ∇log p_{y|t}(y|x_t)。

<span id="S033"></span>
### S033 — heading
**Source:** p.4 S033
**Original:** 3.1 Tweedie moment projections  
**中文：** 3.1 Tweedie 矩投影

<span id="S034"></span>
### S034 — paragraph
**Source:** p.4 S034
**Original:** Instead of just approximating the variance of p0|t(x0|xt) heuristically, we approximate it by projecting onto the closest Gaussian distribution using Tweedie’s formula for the second moment. Our approximation at this stage consists of two main steps: (i) Find the mean and covariance of p0|t(x0|xt) using Tweedie’s formula, and (ii) approximate this density with a Gaussian using the mean and covariance of p0|t(x0|xt) (moment projection). Due to this approximation, we will refer to the resulting methods as Tweedie Moment Projected Diffusions (TMPD). We will first introduce Tweedie’s formula for the mean and covariance and then describe the moment projection.  
**中文：** 我们不再仅以启发式方式近似 p_{0|t}(x₀|x_t) 的方差，而是使用 Tweedie 公式给出的二阶矩，将其投影到距离最近的高斯分布上。此处的近似分为两个主要步骤：（i）利用 Tweedie 公式求出 p_{0|t}(x₀|x_t) 的均值和协方差；（ii）使用这些均值和协方差，以高斯分布近似该密度（矩投影）。基于这一近似，我们将所得方法称为 Tweedie Moment Projected Diffusions（TMPD）。下文先介绍均值与协方差形式的 Tweedie 公式，再说明矩投影。

<span id="S035"></span>
### S035 — proposition
**Source:** p.4 S035
**Original:** Proposition 1 (Tweedie’s formula). Let \(\mathbf{m}_{0|t}\) and \(\mathbf{C}_{0|t}\) be the mean and the covariance of \(p_{0|t}(\mathbf{x}_0|\mathbf{x}_t)\), respectively. Then given the marginal density \(p_t(\mathbf{x}_t)\), the mean is given as  
**中文：** 命题1（Tweedie 公式）。设 \(\mathbf{m}_{0|t}\) 和 \(\mathbf{C}_{0|t}\) 分别为 \(p_{0|t}(\mathbf{x}_0|\mathbf{x}_t)\) 的均值与协方差。给定边缘密度 \(p_t(\mathbf{x}_t)\)，其均值为：

<span id="S036"></span>
### S036 — equation
**Source:** p.4 S036
**Original:** \(\mathbf{m}_{0|t}=\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{x}_0|\mathbf{x}_t]=\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_t}}(\mathbf{x}_t+v_t\nabla_{\mathbf{x}_t}\log p_t(\mathbf{x}_t)).\) (5)  
**中文：** 均值公式（5）保留原符号与编号：\[\mathbf{m}_{0|t}=\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{x}_0|\mathbf{x}_t]=\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_t}}\left(\mathbf{x}_t+v_t\nabla_{\mathbf{x}_t}\log p_t(\mathbf{x}_t)\right).\]

<span id="S037"></span>
### S037 — equation
**Source:** p.4 S037
**Original:** \(\mathbf{C}_{0|t}=\mathbb{E}[(\mathbf{x}_0-\mathbf{m}_{0|t})(\mathbf{x}_0-\mathbf{m}_{0|t})^\top|\mathbf{x}_t]=\frac{v_t}{\alpha_t}(\mathbf{I}_{d_x}+v_t\nabla^2\log p_t(\mathbf{x}_t))=\frac{v_t}{\sqrt{\alpha_t}}\nabla_{\mathbf{x}_t}\mathbf{m}_{0|t}.\) (6)  
**中文：** 协方差公式（6）保留转置、矩阵项、导数阶数和编号：\[\mathbf{C}_{0|t}=\mathbb{E}\!\left[(\mathbf{x}_0-\mathbf{m}_{0|t})(\mathbf{x}_0-\mathbf{m}_{0|t})^\top\mid\mathbf{x}_t\right]=\frac{v_t}{\alpha_t}\left(\mathbf{I}_{d_x}+v_t\nabla^2\log p_t(\mathbf{x}_t)\right)=\frac{v_t}{\sqrt{\alpha_t}}\nabla_{\mathbf{x}_t}\mathbf{m}_{0|t}.\]

<span id="S038"></span>
### S038 — paragraph
**Source:** p.4 S038
**Original:** The proof is an adaptation of Meng et al. (2021, Theorem 1), see Appendix A.1. While m0|t and C0|t give us the moments of the density p0|t(x0|xt), we do not have the exact form of this density. At this stage, we employ moment projection, i.e., we choose the closest Gaussian in Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence which is a distribution with the same first and second moments, as formalised next.  
**中文：** 该证明改编自 Meng et al.（2021，定理1），见附录 A.1。尽管 m_{0|t} 和 C_{0|t} 给出了密度 p_{0|t}(x₀|x_t) 的矩，我们仍不知道该密度的精确形式。此处采用矩投影：在具有相同一阶矩和二阶矩的分布中，选择与该密度之间 Kullback–Leibler（KL）散度最小的高斯分布，具体形式如下。

### S039 — proposition
**Source:** p.5 S039
**Original:** Proposition 2 (Moment projection). Let \(p_{0|t}(\mathbf{x}_0|\mathbf{x}_t)\) be a distribution with mean \(\mathbf{m}_{0|t}\) and covariance \(\mathbf{C}_{0|t}\). Let \(\hat p_{0|t}(\mathbf{x}_0|\mathbf{x}_t)\) be the closest Gaussian in KL divergence to \(p_{0|t}(\mathbf{x}_0|\mathbf{x}_t)\), i.e.,  
**中文：** 命题2（矩投影）。设 \(p_{0|t}(\mathbf{x}_0|\mathbf{x}_t)\) 是一个均值为 \(\mathbf{m}_{0|t}\)、协方差为 \(\mathbf{C}_{0|t}\) 的分布。令 \(\hat p_{0|t}(\mathbf{x}_0|\mathbf{x}_t)\) 表示与 \(p_{0|t}(\mathbf{x}_0|\mathbf{x}_t)\) 的 KL 散度最小的高斯分布，即：

<span id="S040"></span>
### S040 — equation
**Source:** p.5 S040
**Original:** \(\hat p_{0|t}(\mathbf{x}_0|\mathbf{x}_t)=\arg\min_{q\in\mathcal{Q}}D_{\mathrm{KL}}(p_{0|t}(\mathbf{x}_0|\mathbf{x}_t)\|q).\) (7)  
**中文：** 式（7）保留 KL 散度方向、优化变量和编号：\[\hat p_{0|t}(\mathbf{x}_0|\mathbf{x}_t)=\arg\min_{q\in\mathcal{Q}}D_{\mathrm{KL}}\!\left(p_{0|t}(\mathbf{x}_0|\mathbf{x}_t)\|q\right).\]

<span id="S041"></span>
### S041 — paragraph_equation

**Original:** where \(\mathcal{Q}\) is the family of multivariate Gaussian distributions. Then \(\hat p_{0|t}(\mathbf{x}_0|\mathbf{x}_t)=\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{x}_0;\mathbf{m}_{0|t},\mathbf{C}_{0|t}).\) (8)  
**中文：** 其中，\(\mathcal{Q}\) 是多元高斯分布族。于是 \[\hat p_{0|t}(\mathbf{x}_0|\mathbf{x}_t)=\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{x}_0;\mathbf{m}_{0|t},\mathbf{C}_{0|t}).\] 式号为（8）。

<span id="S044"></span>
### S044 — heading
**Source:** p.5 S044
**Original:** 3.2 Tweedie Moment Projected Likelihood Approximation  
**中文：** 3.2 Tweedie 矩投影似然近似

<span id="S045"></span>
### S045 — equation
**Source:** p.5 S045
**Original:** \(p_{y|t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x}_t)\approx\int p_{y|0}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x}_0)\hat p_{0|t}(\mathbf{x}_0|\mathbf{x}_t)\,d\mathbf{x}_0=\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{y};\mathbf{H}\mathbf{m}_{0|t},\mathbf{H}\mathbf{C}_{0|t}\mathbf{H}^\top+\sigma_y^2\mathbf{I}_{d_y}).\) (10)  
**中文：** 将式（9）的近似代入可解析计算的积分，得到式（10）；其中观测均值与噪声协方差项保持原样：\[p_{y|t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x}_t)\approx\int p_{y|0}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x}_0)\hat p_{0|t}(\mathbf{x}_0|\mathbf{x}_t)\,\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}_0=\mathcal{N}\!\left(\mathbf{y};\mathbf{H}\mathbf{m}_{0|t},\mathbf{H}\mathbf{C}_{0|t}\mathbf{H}^\top+\sigma_y^2\mathbf{I}_{d_y}\right).\]

<span id="S046"></span>
### S046 — paragraph
**Source:** p.5 S046
**Original:** Let us recall that mean and covariance terms are a function of xt by making them explicit in the notation, i.e., m0|t(xt) and C0|t(xt) for the mean and covariance respectively. To compute ∇xt log py|t(y|xt), we require further approximations since we have xt dependence in both the mean and the covariance of Equation (10) which is computationally infeasible to differentiate through. For this reason, we treat the matrix C0|t like a constant w.r.t. xt when computing the gradient (which is the case if pdata is Gaussian). For non-Gaussian pdata, this results in a computationally efficient sampler using C0|t as a preconditioner for the step size, as otherwise, the resulting terms can be expensive to compute. This leads to an approximation of the gradient  
**中文：** 为明确表示依赖关系，均值和协方差分别写作 m_{0|t}(x_t) 与 C_{0|t}(x_t)。计算 ∇_{x_t}log p_{y|t}(y|x_t) 时还需作进一步近似，因为式（10）中的均值和协方差都依赖 x_t，若通过这两项求导，计算上难以承受。因此，计算梯度时将矩阵 C_{0|t} 视为相对于 x_t 的常量（当 p_data 为高斯分布时确实如此）。对于非高斯 p_data，这样得到的采样器计算效率较高，并将 C_{0|t} 用作步长的预条件矩阵；否则，相关项的计算代价会很高。由此得到如下梯度近似：

<span id="S047"></span>
### S047 — equation
**Source:** p.5 S047
**Original:** \(f^y(\mathbf{x}_t):=\nabla_{\mathbf{x}_t}\mathbf{m}_{0|t}(\mathbf{x}_t)\mathbf{H}^\top(\mathbf{H}\mathbf{C}_{0|t}(\mathbf{x}_t)\mathbf{H}^\top+\sigma_y^2\mathbf{I}_{d_y})^{-1}(\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{H}\mathbf{m}_{0|t}(\mathbf{x}_t))\approx\nabla_{\mathbf{x}_t}\log p_{y|t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x}_t).\) (11)  
**中文：** 式（11）保留矩阵乘法次序、逆矩阵、残差项和编号：\[f^y(\mathbf{x}_t):=\nabla_{\mathbf{x}_t}\mathbf{m}_{0|t}(\mathbf{x}_t)\mathbf{H}^\top\!\left(\mathbf{H}\mathbf{C}_{0|t}(\mathbf{x}_t)\mathbf{H}^\top+\sigma_y^2\mathbf{I}_{d_y}\right)^{-1}\!\left(\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{H}\mathbf{m}_{0|t}(\mathbf{x}_t)\right)\approx\nabla_{\mathbf{x}_t}\log p_{y|t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x}_t).\]

<span id="S048"></span>
### S048 — equation
**Source:** p.5 S048
**Original:** where \(\nabla_{\mathbf{x}_t}\) only operates on \(\mathbf{m}_{0|t}\) in Equation (11). Another way to write this approximation is to use Equation (6), which leads to \(f^y(\mathbf{x}_t):=\frac{\sqrt{\alpha_t}}{v_t}\mathbf{C}_{0|t}(\mathbf{x}_t)\mathbf{H}^\top(\mathbf{H}\mathbf{C}_{0|t}(\mathbf{x}_t)\mathbf{H}^\top+\sigma_y^2\mathbf{I}_{d_y})^{-1}(\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{H}\mathbf{m}_{0|t}(\mathbf{x}_t)).\)  
**中文：** 式（11）中的 \(\nabla_{\mathbf{x}_t}\) 只作用于 \(\mathbf{m}_{0|t}\)。利用式（6）还可将该近似写为：\[f^y(\mathbf{x}_t):=\frac{\sqrt{\alpha_t}}{v_t}\mathbf{C}_{0|t}(\mathbf{x}_t)\mathbf{H}^\top\!\left(\mathbf{H}\mathbf{C}_{0|t}(\mathbf{x}_t)\mathbf{H}^\top+\sigma_y^2\mathbf{I}_{d_y}\right)^{-1}\!\left(\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{H}\mathbf{m}_{0|t}(\mathbf{x}_t)\right).\]

<span id="S049"></span>
### S049 — heading
**Source:** p.5 S049
**Original:** 3.3 Algorithms  
**中文：** 3.3 算法

<span id="S050"></span>
### S050 — paragraph
**Source:** p.5 S050
**Original:** Plugging the approximation in Equation (11) into the reverse SDE in Equation (2) together with the prior score as described in Section 2.1 results in a TMPD for conditional sampling to solve inverse problems. The SDE we will approximate numerically to sample from the conditional distribution is given by  
**中文：** 将式（11）的近似与第2.1节所述先验 score 一并代入式（2）的反向 SDE，可得到用于逆问题条件采样的 TMPD。为从条件分布采样，需要数值近似求解的 SDE 为：

<span id="S051"></span>
### S051 — equation
**Source:** p.5 S051
**Original:** \(d\mathbf{z}_t=\frac12\beta(T-t)\mathbf{z}_t\,dt+\beta(T-t)(\nabla_{\mathbf{z}_t}\log p_{T-t}(\mathbf{z}_t)+f^y_{T-t}(\mathbf{z}_t))\,dt+\sqrt{\beta(T-t)}\,d\bar{\mathbf{w}}_t.\) (12)  
**中文：** TMPD SDE 的式（12）保留漂移项、似然 score 修正项、扩散项和编号：\[\mathrm{d}\mathbf{z}_t=\frac12\beta(T-t)\mathbf{z}_t\,\mathrm{d}t+\beta(T-t)\left(\nabla_{\mathbf{z}_t}\log p_{T-t}(\mathbf{z}_t)+f^y_{T-t}(\mathbf{z}_t)\right)\,\mathrm{d}t+\sqrt{\beta(T-t)}\,\mathrm{d}\bar{\mathbf{w}}_t.\]

<span id="S052"></span>
### S052 — paragraph
**Source:** pp.5–6 S052
**Original:** where \(\mathbf{z}_0\sim p_T\) and \(f^y_t(\mathbf{z}_t)\approx\nabla\log p_{y|T-t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{z}_t)\) is our approximation to the data likelihood, given by Equation (11). We call this SDE the TMPD SDE. We have two options to convert TMPD SDE into implementable methods: (1) score-based samplers (Song & Ermon, 2020), which we abbreviate as TMPD since they are Euler-Maruyama discretizations of the TMPD SDE; and (2) denoising diffusion models (TMPD-D).  
**中文：** 其中 \(\mathbf{z}_0\sim p_T\)，且 \(f^y_t(\mathbf{z}_t)\approx\nabla\log p_{y|T-t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{z}_t)\) 是对数据似然 score 的近似，其表达式见式（11）。我们将该 SDE 称为 TMPD SDE。将 TMPD SDE 转换为可实现方法有两种选择：（1）基于 score 的采样器（Song & Ermon, 2020），简称 TMPD，因为它们是对 TMPD SDE 采用 Euler–Maruyama 离散化的采样器；（2）去噪扩散模型（TMPD-D）。

### S053 — paragraph
**Source:** p.6 S053
**Original:** The denoising diffusion approach is derived from approximate reverse Markov chains and is the approach of DDPM and DPS methods (Ho et al., 2020; Chung et al., 2022a). We note that the Gaussian projection can be used in this discrete setting, assuming that the conditional density is available analytically as in Ho et al. (2020), and can be written as \(p_{n|0}(\mathbf{x}_n|\mathbf{x}_0)=\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{x}_n;\sqrt{\alpha_n}\mathbf{x}_0,v_n\mathbf{I}_{d_x}).\)  
**中文：** 去噪扩散方法源自近似反向马尔可夫链，也是 DDPM 和 DPS 方法所采用的思路（Ho et al., 2020; Chung et al., 2022a）。作者指出，在条件密度可解析获得的离散设置中也可以使用高斯投影，例如 Ho et al. (2020) 的情形。此时转移分布可写为 \(p_{n|0}(\mathbf{x}_n|\mathbf{x}_0)=\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{x}_n;\sqrt{\alpha_n}\mathbf{x}_0,v_n\mathbf{I}_{d_x})\)。

<span id="S054"></span>
### S054 — paragraph
**Source:** p.6 S054
**Original:** The idea is to update the unconditional mean \(\mathbf{m}_{0|n}(\mathbf{x}_n)\) of the density \(p_{0|n}(\mathbf{x}_0|\mathbf{x}_n)\) with a Bayesian update: \(p(\mathbf{x}_0|\mathbf{x}_n,\mathbf{y})\propto p(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x}_n)p_{0|n}(\mathbf{x}_0|\mathbf{x}_n).\) Given a similar formulation as above, assuming we have a readily available approximation \(p_{0|n}(\mathbf{x}_0|\mathbf{x}_n)\approx\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{x}_0;\mathbf{m}_{0|n},\mathbf{C}_{0|n})\) and a likelihood similar to Equation (10) where t can be replaced by n, we can compute the moments of \(p(\mathbf{x}_0|\mathbf{x}_n,\mathbf{y})\) analytically, which we denote \(\mathbf{m}^{\mathbf{y}}_{0|n}\) and \(\mathbf{C}^{\mathbf{y}}_{0|n}\). The Bayes update for Gaussians gives (Bishop, 2006)  
**中文：** 其思路是对密度 \(p_{0|n}(\mathbf{x}_0|\mathbf{x}_n)\) 的无条件均值 \(\mathbf{m}_{0|n}(\mathbf{x}_n)\) 作贝叶斯更新：\(p(\mathbf{x}_0|\mathbf{x}_n,\mathbf{y})\propto p(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x}_n)p_{0|n}(\mathbf{x}_0|\mathbf{x}_n)\)。采用与前文相似的设定，假设已有近似 \(p_{0|n}(\mathbf{x}_0|\mathbf{x}_n)\approx\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{x}_0;\mathbf{m}_{0|n},\mathbf{C}_{0|n})\)，且似然形式与式（10）相似、只需将 t 换为 n，则可以解析计算 \(p(\mathbf{x}_0|\mathbf{x}_n,\mathbf{y})\) 的矩，分别记为 \(\mathbf{m}^{\mathbf{y}}_{0|n}\) 和 \(\mathbf{C}^{\mathbf{y}}_{0|n}\)。高斯分布的贝叶斯更新为下式（Bishop, 2006）：

<span id="S055"></span>
### S055 — equation
**Source:** p.6 S055
**Original:** \(\mathbf{m}^{\mathbf{y}}_{0|n}=\mathbf{m}_{0|n}+\mathbf{C}_{0|n}\mathbf{H}^\top(\mathbf{H}\mathbf{C}_{0|n}\mathbf{H}^\top+\sigma_y^2\mathbf{I}_{d_x})^{-1}(\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{H}\mathbf{m}_{0|n}).\) (13)  
**中文：** 均值贝叶斯更新式（13）按原文保留；原文单位阵下标为 \(d_x\)，照录如下：\[\mathbf{m}^{\mathbf{y}}_{0|n}=\mathbf{m}_{0|n}+\mathbf{C}_{0|n}\mathbf{H}^\top\!\left(\mathbf{H}\mathbf{C}_{0|n}\mathbf{H}^\top+\sigma_y^2\mathbf{I}_{d_x}\right)^{-1}\!\left(\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{H}\mathbf{m}_{0|n}\right).\]

<span id="S056"></span>
### S056 — paragraph
**Source:** p.6 S056
**Original:** Incorporating Equation (13) for n=N−1,...,0 into the usual Ancestral sampling (Ho et al., 2020) steps leads to Algorithm 1, termed TMPD-Denoising (TPMD-D). The update in Equation (13) can be used in any discrete sampler such as denoising diffusion implicit models (DDIM) (Song et al., 2021a).  
**中文：** 在通常的祖先采样步骤（Ancestral sampling）（Ho et al., 2020）中，对 n=N−1,...,0 逐步使用式（13），即可得到算法1，文中称其为 TMPD-Denoising（TPMD-D）。式（13）的更新可用于任意离散采样器，例如去噪扩散隐式模型（DDIM）（Song et al., 2021a）。

<span id="S057"></span>
### S057 — heading
**Source:** p.6 S057
**Original:** 3.4 Computationally cheaper approximation of Moment Projection  
**中文：** 3.4 计算代价更低的矩投影近似

<span id="S058"></span>
### S058 — paragraph
**Source:** p.6 S058
**Original:** We show in our experiments promising results for TMPD motivating the exploration of less computationally expensive approximations to the full Jacobian. In particular, we empirically study a computationally inexpensive method that applies to inpainting and super-resolution, below.  
**中文：** 实验中 TMPD 展现出较好的结果，这促使作者进一步探索完整雅可比矩阵的低计算量近似。具体而言，下文将通过实验证明一种计算开销较低、适用于图像修补和超分辨率的方法。

<span id="S059"></span>
### S059 — paragraph
**Source:** p.6 S059
**Original:** To make the computational cost of TMPD smaller, we can make an approximation of the Gaussian Projection that requires fewer vector-Jacobian products and does not require linear solves. One approximation that we found useful for sampling from high dimensional diffusion models, e.g., high resolution images, is denoted here as diagonal Tweedie Moment Projection (DTMPD). Instead of the full second moment, DTMPD uses the diagonal of the second moment \(\nabla_{\mathbf{x}_t}\mathbf{m}_{0|t}\approx\operatorname{diag}(\nabla_{\mathbf{x}_t}\mathbf{m}_{0|t}).\) Intuitively, this approximation will perform well empirically since it is a similar approximation to ΠGDM that assumes dimensional independence of the distribution \(p(\mathbf{x}_0|\mathbf{x}_t)\), but unlike ΠGDM, this diagonal approximation is the same as using the closest dimensionally independent Gaussian in KL divergence to \(p_{0|t}(\mathbf{x}_0|\mathbf{x}_t)\).  
**中文：** 为降低 TMPD 的计算开销，可以近似高斯投影，从而减少向量–雅可比积（vector-Jacobian product）次数，并避免线性方程求解。本文将一种适用于高维扩散模型采样（例如高分辨率图像）的近似称为对角 Tweedie 矩投影（DTMPD）。DTMPD 不使用完整二阶矩，而使用其对角近似：\(\nabla_{\mathbf{x}_t}\mathbf{m}_{0|t}\approx\operatorname{diag}(\nabla_{\mathbf{x}_t}\mathbf{m}_{0|t})\)。直观上，该近似在经验上可能表现良好，因为它与 ΠGDM 类似，都假设分布 \(p(\mathbf{x}_0|\mathbf{x}_t)\) 的各维度独立；但与 ΠGDM 不同，这种对角近似等价于在 KL 散度意义下，选取距离 \(p_{0|t}(\mathbf{x}_0|\mathbf{x}_t)\) 最近的维度独立高斯分布。

<span id="S060"></span>
### S060 — paragraph
**Source:** p.6 S060
**Original:** The biggest drawback of our method is that without further approximation, it doesn’t scale up to the high dimensions of image data. This is because even calculating the diagonal of a Jacobian requires computing dx vector-Jacobian products since in general every element of the Jacobian at a location xt, ∇xt m0|t depends on every element of xt. Therefore we must resort to a further approximation that exploits knowledge of the observation operator H.  
**中文：** 该方法最大的缺点是，若不作进一步近似，就无法扩展到图像数据的高维规模。这是因为即使只计算雅可比矩阵的对角线，一般也需要计算 d_x 个向量–雅可比积：在位置 x_t 处，雅可比矩阵 ∇_{x_t}m_{0|t} 的每个元素通常都依赖于 x_t 的每个元素。因此，需要进一步利用观测算子 H 的结构作近似。

<span id="S061"></span>
### S061 — paragraph
**Source:** p.6 S061
**Original:** For the cases of super-resolution and inpainting, a further approximation that allows scaling up to the dimensions of image data is approximating the diagonal of the Jacobian by the row sum of the Jacobian which only requires a single vector-Jacobian product and brings the memory and time complexity of DTMPD down to that of ΠGDM. We exploit the sparsity of H to make the row sum approximation of the diagonal more accurate by masking out (zeroing) the values in the vector-Jacobian product that will not contribute to the diagonal of HC0|nH⊤. We discuss a justification of this approximation in Appendix E.1. We use this approximation in the image experiments and find that in practice it is only (1.5±0.1)× slower than ΠGDM and DPS across all of our experiments (Section 6), with competitive sample quality for noisy inverse problems and without the need for expensive hyperparameter tuning. Finally, we note a very recent work (Rozet et al., 2024) that circumvents our heuristic by applying the conjugate gradient (CG) method, unlocking using the approximation Eq. 11 to be used in practice for non-sparse H (see Appendix E.1 for more details).  
**中文：** 对于超分辨率和图像修补，作者进一步用雅可比矩阵的行和近似其对角线，以扩展到图像数据规模。该方法只需一次向量–雅可比积，并使 DTMPD 的内存和时间复杂度降至与 ΠGDM 相当。作者利用 H 的稀疏性，通过屏蔽（置零）向量–雅可比积中不会对 HC_{0|n}H^⊤ 对角线产生贡献的元素，提高行和近似的准确性。该近似的依据见附录 E.1。作者在图像实验中使用这一近似，结果显示其实际运行速度仅比 ΠGDM 和 DPS 慢 (1.5±0.1)×；在所有实验中，对于含噪逆问题，该方法取得有竞争力的样本质量，且无需昂贵的超参数调节。最后，作者提到一项近期工作（Rozet et al., 2024），通过共轭梯度（conjugate gradient，CG）方法绕过本文启发式处理，使式（11）的近似能够用于非稀疏 H 的实际场景（更多细节见附录 E.1）。

<span id="S062"></span>
### S062 — heading
**Source:** p.6 S062
**Original:** 4 Theoretical Guarantees  
**中文：** 4 理论保证

<span id="S063"></span>
### S063 — paragraph
**Source:** pp.6–7 S063
**Original:** Because of the approximations, our method, as well as ΠGDM (Song et al., 2023) and DPS (Chung et al., 2022a) do not sample the exact posterior for general prior distributions. Therefore, one cannot hope for these methods to sample the true posterior and a priori it is not even clear how the sampled distribution relates to the true posterior. Without further justification, such methods should only be interpreted as guidance methods, where paths are guided to regions where a given observation y is more likely, not as posterior sampling methods.  
**中文：** 由于存在近似，对于一般先验分布，本文方法以及 ΠGDM（Song et al., 2023）和 DPS（Chung et al., 2022a）都不能从精确后验中采样。因此，不能期待这些方法采得真实后验；并且在缺乏先验依据的情况下，采样分布与真实后验之间的关系也并不清楚。若无进一步论证，这些方法只能被视为引导方法，其中采样路径被引导至给定观测 y 更可能出现的区域；这类方法并非后验采样方法。

**Original:** We justify our approximation by showing that the TMPD-SDE in Equation (12) is able to sample the exact posterior in the Gaussian case. One can see that this contrasts with ΠGDM and DPS in our numerical experiments or by explicitly evaluating their approximations on simple one-dimensional examples.  
**中文：** 作者通过证明式（12）的 TMPD-SDE 在高斯情形下能够从精确后验采样，为所提近似提供依据。通过数值实验，或显式计算简单一维示例中的近似，也可以看出这一性质与 ΠGDM 和 DPS 的区别。

<span id="S065"></span>
### S065 — proposition
**Source:** p.7 S065
**Original:** Proposition 3 (Gaussian data distribution). Assume that \(p_{\mathrm{data}}\) is Gaussian. Then, the posterior score expression using Equation (11) is exact, i.e., if there are no errors in the initial condition and drift approximation \(s_\theta(\mathbf{x}_t,t)=\nabla_{\mathbf{x}_t}\log p_t(\mathbf{x}_t)\), the TMPD will sample \(p_{\mathrm{data}}(\cdot|\mathbf{y})\) at its final time.  
**中文：** 命题3（高斯数据分布）。假设 \(p_{\mathrm{data}}\) 为高斯分布，则使用式（11）的后验 score 表达式是精确的。也就是说，若初始条件和漂移近似均无误差，且 \(s_\theta(\mathbf{x}_t,t)=\nabla_{\mathbf{x}_t}\log p_t(\mathbf{x}_t)\)，则 TMPD 在终止时刻会从 \(p_{\mathrm{data}}(\cdot|\mathbf{y})\) 中采样。

<span id="S079"></span>
### S079 — paragraph
**Source:** p.7 S079
**Original:** The choice of \(r_t\) is such that it matches the variance of the reverse SDE if \(p_{\mathrm{data}}\) would be a standard normal distribution. Since they employ a different forward SDE to ours (variance exploding SDE), \(r_t\) is set, when the variance of the data is 1, to be equal to \(v_t/(v_t+1)\). In our case, with the OU/variance preserving SDE as a forward process, \(r_t\) would be equal to \(v_t/(v_t+\alpha_t)\), when the variance of the data is 1.  
**中文：** 参数 \(r_t\) 的选取使得当 \(p_{\mathrm{data}}\) 为标准正态分布时，它与反向 SDE 的方差相匹配。由于该方法采用的前向 SDE（方差爆炸 SDE）与本文不同，当数据方差为1时，取 \(r_t=v_t/(v_t+1)\)。而本文采用 OU／方差保持 SDE 作为前向过程；在数据方差为1时，对应 \(r_t=v_t/(v_t+\alpha_t)\)。

<span id="S080"></span>
### S080 — footnote
**Source:** p.7 S080
**Original:** Since the authors run DDPM-type sampler, we re-abbreviate DPS as DPS-D in this work (as DPS approximation itself can also be run with Euler-Maruyama schemes).  
**中文：** 由于原作者运行的是 DDPM 类型采样器，本文将 DPS 重新简称为 DPS-D（DPS 近似本身也可以通过 Euler–Maruyama 方法运行）。

<span id="S081"></span>
### S081 — footnote
**Source:** p.7 S081
**Original:** Since the authors run DDIM-type sampler, we re-abbreviate ΠGDM as ΠGDM-D in this work (as ΠGDM approximation itself can also be run with Euler-Maruyama schemes).  
**中文：** 由于原作者运行的是 DDIM 类型采样器，本文将 ΠGDM 重新简称为 ΠGDM-D（ΠGDM 近似本身也可以通过 Euler–Maruyama 方法运行）。

## Page 8

<span id="page-8"></span>
**Page status:** Complete

<span id="S082"></span>
### S082 — paragraph
**Source:** p.8 S082
**Original:** Another relevant very recent work by Finzi et al. (2023) arrives at the approximation matching ours in the context of modelling physical constraints. However, we focus on general linear inverse problems outside the physical domain in this paper together with a novel theoretical result. Also, we note the work of Stevens et al. (2023) who consider the maximum-a-posteriori (MAP) approach to find the moments of p0|t.  
**中文：** Finzi et al. (2023）的一项相关近期工作在物理约束建模背景下也得到了与本文相同的近似。不过，本文关注物理领域之外的一般线性逆问题，并给出新的理论结果。此外，Stevens et al. (2023）研究了通过最大后验（maximum a posteriori，MAP）方法求取 p_{0|t} 的矩。

<span id="S083"></span>
### S083 — paragraph
**Source:** p.8 S083
**Original:** Finally, sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) methods are particle-based posterior sampling schemes that are asymptotically exact in the limit of infinitely many particles. Cardoso et al. (2023) target the posterior for linear inverse problems, and our first experiment makes a comparison to Cardoso et al. (2023) for a Gaussian Mixture model. However, for high dimensional data (such as images), this method can exhibit the well studied SMC problem of weight degeneracy, where particle weights collapse. We note a recent state of the art work (Wu et al., 2024) that tackles weight degeneracy by introducing the Twisted Diffusion Sampler (TDS), with heuristic twisting functions approximating py|t(y|xt) that increase computational efficiency of SMC. The method is still asymptotically exact as long as the approximation of py|t(y|xt) converges to p(y|x0) as t approaches 0. The authors note that the efficiency of TDS (Wu et al., 2024) depends on how closely the twisting function approximates the exact likelihood. TMPD (Equation (10)) could be a good twisting candidate.  
**中文：** 最后，序贯蒙特卡洛（sequential Monte Carlo，SMC）是基于粒子的后验采样方法；当粒子数趋于无穷时，它在渐近意义上精确。Cardoso et al. (2023) 针对线性逆问题中的后验分布，本文首个实验还与 Cardoso et al. (2023) 在高斯混合模型上的结果进行比较。不过，对于图像等高维数据，该方法可能出现 SMC 中已知的权重退化问题，即粒子权重集中塌缩。近期一项前沿工作（Wu et al., 2024）通过引入扭曲扩散采样器（Twisted Diffusion Sampler，TDS）应对权重退化，其中启发式扭曲函数近似 \(p_{y|t}(y|x_t)\)，以提高 SMC 的计算效率。只要 \(p_{y|t}(y|x_t)\) 的近似在 \(t\to0\) 时收敛到 \(p(y|x_0)\)，该方法仍具有渐近精确性。作者指出，TDS（Wu et al., 2024）的效率取决于扭曲函数与精确似然的接近程度。TMPD（式（10））可能是一个合适的扭曲候选。

<span id="S084"></span>
### S084 — heading
**Source:** p.8 S084
**Original:** 6 Experiments  
**中文：** 6 实验

<span id="S085"></span>
### S085 — paragraph
**Source:** p.8 S085
**Original:** In this section, we demonstrate our results as well as the performance of other approximations to the likelihood provided in Chung et al. (2022a); Song et al. (2023). In particular, we perform comparisons for two of our methods TMPD (an SGM using our approximation) and TMPD-D (a DDPM sampler using Equation (13)). We compare these to DPS (an SGM sampler using the posterior approximation in Equation (16)), DPS-D (Chung et al., 2022a) (a DDPM-type sampler using Equation (16)), ΠGDM (Song et al., 2023) (an SGM sampler using the posterior approximation in Equation (17)), and finally ΠGDM-D (a DDIM-type sampler using Equation (17), but in our experiments a DDPM-type sampler since we set the DDIM hyperparameter η=1.0 which is defined in Algorithm 1 by Song et al. (2021a) who show that this is equivalent to a DDPM-type sampler).  
**中文：** 本节展示本文结果，并评估 Chung et al. (2022a）和 Song et al. (2023）提出的其他似然近似。具体比较本文的两种方法：TMPD（采用所提近似的 SGM）和 TMPD-D（使用式（13）的 DDPM 采样器）；并与以下方法比较：DPS（使用式（16）后验近似的 SGM 采样器）、DPS-D（Chung et al., 2022a，使用式（16）的 DDPM 类型采样器）、ΠGDM（Song et al., 2023，使用式（17）后验近似的 SGM 采样器），以及 ΠGDM-D（使用式（17）的 DDIM 类型采样器）。在本文实验中，ΠGDM-D 实际采用 DDPM 类型采样器，因为 DDIM 超参数 η 设为 1.0；Song et al. (2021a）在算法1中定义了这一参数，并指出此设置等价于 DDPM 类型采样器。

**Original:** 6.1 Gaussian Mixture Model  
**中文：** 6.1 高斯混合模型

<span id="S088"></span>
### S088 — paragraph
**Source:** p.8 S088
**Original:** We now demonstrate a nonlinear SDE example and follow the Gaussian mixture model example of Cardoso et al. (2023) where the data distribution p0(x0) is a mixture of 25 Gaussian distributions. The means and variances of the components of the mixture are given in Appendix E.3. In this case, for each choice of observation y, observation map H and measurement noise standard deviation σy, the target posterior can be computed explicitly (see Appendix E.3).  
**中文：** 下面展示一个非线性 SDE 示例，并沿用 Cardoso et al. (2023）的高斯混合模型设定，其中数据分布 p_0(x_0) 是由25个高斯分布构成的混合分布。各混合分量的均值和方差见附录 E.3。在该设置下，对于每组观测 y、观测映射 H 和测量噪声标准差 σ_y，都可以显式计算目标后验（见附录 E.3）。

<span id="S089"></span>
### S089 — paragraph
**Source:** p.8 S089
**Original:** To investigate the performance of posterior sampling methods, for each pair of dimensions and observation noise \((d_x,d_y,\sigma_y)\in\{8,80,800\}\times\{1,2,4\}\times\{10^{-2},10^{-1},10^0\}\) we randomly generate multiple measurement models \((\mathbf{y},\mathbf{H})\in\mathbb{R}^{d_y}\times\mathbb{R}^{d_y\times d_x}\), and equally weight each component of the Gaussian mixture. Further details are given in Appendix E.3. We chose to control the dimension to gain insight into the performance of posterior sampling methods under varying dimensions. We also chose to control the noise level since the different posterior sampling methods have accuracy that depends on the signal-to-noise ratio. Through randomly varying the observation model, we gain an insight into the performance of the posterior sampling methods with different levels of posterior multimodality. This example is interesting because it allows us to study the behaviour of our methods on non-Gaussian problems in high dimensions whilst having access to the target posterior with which to compare (usually, obtaining a ‘ground-truth’ posterior is not feasible for non-Gaussian problems).  
**中文：** 为研究后验采样方法的性能，针对每组维度与观测噪声参数 \((d_x,d_y,\sigma_y)\in\{8,80,800\}\times\{1,2,4\}\times\{10^{-2},10^{-1},10^0\}\)，作者随机生成多个测量模型 \((\mathbf{y},\mathbf{H})\in\mathbb{R}^{d_y}\times\mathbb{R}^{d_y\times d_x}\)，并对高斯混合中的每个分量赋予相同权重。更多细节见附录 E.3。作者控制问题维度，以观察后验采样方法在不同维度下的性能；也控制噪声水平，因为不同方法的准确性依赖于信噪比。通过随机改变观测模型，可以考察后验多模态程度不同时各方法的表现。该示例有助于研究这些方法在高维非高斯问题上的行为，同时又能获得目标后验作为比较基准（通常非高斯问题难以得到“真实值”后验）。

<span id="S090"></span>
### S090 — paragraph
**Source:** p.8 S090
**Original:** We use the sliced Wasserstein (SW) distance defined in Appendix E.3 to compare the posterior distribution estimated by each algorithm with the target posterior distribution. We use 10^4 slices for the SW distance and compare 1000 samples of TMPD-D, ΠGDM-D and DPS-D in Tables 1 obtained using 1000 denoising steps and 1000 samples of the true posterior distribution.  
**中文：** 作者采用附录 E.3 定义的切片 Wasserstein（sliced Wasserstein，SW）距离，将各算法估计的后验分布与目标后验分布比较。计算 SW 距离时使用 10^4 个切片；表1比较了 TMPD-D、ΠGDM-D 和 DPS-D 的1000个样本，这些样本使用1000个去噪步骤生成，并与真实后验分布的1000个样本进行比较。

<span id="S096"></span>
### S096 — paragraph
**Source:** p.9 S096
**Original:** We consider inpainting and super-resolution problems on the FFHQ 256 × 256 (Karras et al., 2019) and CIFAR-10 32 × 32 (Krizhevsky et al., 2009) datasets. We compare TMPD to ΠGDM and DPS. We also compare score-based diffusion models with their denoising-diffusion counterparts (denoted with suffix, -D).  
**中文：** 作者在 FFHQ 256 × 256（Karras et al., 2019）和 CIFAR-10 32 × 32（Krizhevsky et al., 2009）数据集上研究图像修复与超分辨率问题。比较 TMPD 与 ΠGDM、DPS，并将基于 score 的扩散模型与相应的去噪扩散模型进行比较；后者以“-D”作为后缀。

## Page 10

<span id="page-10"></span>
**Page status:** Complete

<span id="S097"></span>
### S097 — paragraph
**Source:** p.10 S097
**Original:** Firstly, we follow the benchmark used by Chung et al. (2022a) and use a Variance Preserving (VP) SDE, using a DDPM sampler, on FFHQ 256 × 256 using 1k validation images. The pre-trained diffusion model for FFHQ was taken from Chung et al. (2022a) and was used directly without any finetuning. We follow Chung et al. (2022a) and use various forward operators. For super-resolution, we use a downsampling ratio of 4 (256 × 256 → 64 × 64) and bicubic interpolation; for ‘box’ mask inpainting we mask out 128 × 128 region and for ‘random’ mask inpainting we choose a random mask for each image masking between 30% and 70% of the pixels. Images are normalized to the range [0, 1] and it is on this scale that we add Gaussian measurement noise with standard deviation σ_y ∈ {0.01, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2}. For quantitative comparison, we focus on two widely used perception distances, Fréchet Inception Distance (FID) and Learned Perceptual Image Patch Similarity (LPIPS) distance. FID evaluates consistency with the whole dataset using summary statistics from the FFHQ-50k dataset. We also evaluate observation data similarity using various distances between a sampled image and ground truth image: LPIPS, mean-squared-error (MSE), peak signal-to-noise-ratio (PSNR) and structural similarity index measure (SSIM). For ΠGDM-D we use the algorithm and default hyperparameters as described in Song et al. (2023). For DPS-D we use the algorithm in the codebase provided by the authors Chung et al. (2022a) and we use their default hyperparameters such as their suggested step-size hyperparameter for this task, and static-thresholding (clipping the denoised image at each step to a range [−1, 1]) whereas TMPD-D does not require hyperparameter tuning or static-thresholding. The results for FFHQ sampled using VP DDPM are shown in the appendix Table 7. We observe that DTMPD-D is competitive with DPD-D over a range of noise levels, however, ΠGDM-D is not able to produce high quality reconstructions for larger noise levels.  
**中文：** 首先，作者沿用 Chung et al. (2022a) 的基准，在 FFHQ 256 × 256 数据集上使用方差保持（Variance Preserving，VP）SDE 和 DDPM 采样器，并采用1000张验证图像。FFHQ 预训练扩散模型取自 Chung et al. (2022a)，直接使用，未作微调。作者遵循 Chung et al. (2022a) 的设置，采用多种前向算子。超分辨率任务使用4倍下采样（256 × 256 → 64 × 64）和双三次插值；“box”掩模修复遮挡一个128 × 128区域；“random”掩模修复则为每张图像随机生成掩模，遮挡30%至70%的像素。图像归一化到 [0, 1]，并在此数值范围内加入标准差 σ_y ∈ {0.01, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2} 的高斯测量噪声。定量比较聚焦于两种常用感知距离：Fréchet Inception Distance（FID）和 Learned Perceptual Image Patch Similarity（LPIPS）距离。FID 使用 FFHQ-50k 数据集的汇总统计量评估结果与整个数据集的一致性。作者还采用生成图像与真实图像之间的多种距离评价观测数据相似性：LPIPS、均方误差（MSE）、峰值信噪比（PSNR）和结构相似性指数度量（SSIM）。ΠGDM-D 使用 Song et al. (2023) 所述算法及其默认超参数。DPS-D 使用 Chung et al. (2022a) 作者代码库中的算法及默认超参数，包括该任务推荐的步长超参数和静态阈值处理（每一步都将去噪图像裁剪到 [−1, 1]）；TMPD-D 无需调节超参数，也不需要静态阈值处理。采用 VP DDPM 生成的 FFHQ 结果见附录表7。作者观察到，在多个噪声水平下，DTMPD-D 的表现与 DPD-D 具有竞争力；但在较大噪声水平下，ΠGDM-D 无法生成高质量重建结果。

<span id="S098"></span>
### S098 — paragraph
**Source:** p.10 S098
**Original:** We note that the heuristics used in the DPS and ΠGDM implementations have been designed to work with the VP-SDE (DDPM sampler), and therefore the performance may not be robust to the choice of SDE. Fig 14 illustrates this when each method is applied to the VE-SDE on a sample from the FFHQ validation dataset. We next compare performance to TMPD across VP and VE-SDE samplers and a range of noise levels on CIFAR-10 64 × 64 using 1k validation images.  
**中文：** 作者指出，DPS 和 ΠGDM 实现中的启发式设计用于 VP-SDE（DDPM 采样器），因此其性能对 SDE 的选择可能不够稳健。图14展示了这一点：将各方法应用于 FFHQ 验证集的一张样本图像并采用 VE-SDE 时，可以观察到这种差异。随后，作者在 CIFAR-10 64 × 64 上采用1000张验证图像，比较 VP 和 VE-SDE 采样器及多个噪声水平下各方法与 TMPD 的表现。

<span id="S099"></span>
### S099 — paragraph
**Source:** p.10 S099
**Original:** We use pretrained denoising networks for CIFAR-10 that are available here (https://github.com/yang-song/score_sde). For inpainting, we use ‘box’ and ‘half’ mask. For ‘half’-type inpainting, we mask out a 16 × 16 right half region of the image; for box-type inpainting, we mask out an 8 × 8 box region following Cardoso et al. (2023). For super-resolution, we use a downsampling ratio of 2 on each axis (32 × 32 → 16 × 16) with a nearest-neighbour downsampling method; and a downsampling ratio of 4 (32 × 32 → 8 × 8) with bicubic downsampling. Images are normalized to the range [0, 1] and it is on this scale that we add Gaussian measurement noise with standard deviation σ_y ∈ {0.01, 0.05, 0.1}. Whereas no hyperparameters are required for our method, we chose the DPS scale hyperparameter by optimising LPIPS, MSE, PSNR and SSIM on a validation set of 128 images (see Fig. 9 for an example). We found that static thresholding (clipping the denoised image estimate to a range [0, 1] at each sampling step) is critical for the stability and performance of both DPS-D and ΠGDM-D. Stability was noted as a limitation in Chung et al. (2022a), and they suggest that devising methods to stabilize the samplers would be a promising direction of research. We find that our TMPD-D method and the diagonal approximation DTMPD-D is stable across SDE, noise-level and observation maps, without the need for static thresholding. Whilst for VE ΠGDM-D we found the original algorithm in Song et al. (2023) to be stable, for VP ΠGDM-D, the original algorithm, whilst stable for FFHQ, was not stable, even with static-thresholding, for CIFAR-10. We chose to bring ΠGDM-D a step closer to our algorithm by substituting their likelihood score into an Ancestral sampling algorithm, instead of a DDIM algorithm as suggested in Song et al. (2023), which produced stable samples.  
**中文：** 作者使用可从此处获取的 CIFAR-10 预训练去噪网络（[score_sde 代码库](https://github.com/yang-song/score_sde)）。图像修复采用“box”和“half”两种掩模；“half”类型遮挡图像右侧一个16 × 16区域，“box”类型则遵循 Cardoso et al. (2023) 遮挡一个8 × 8区域。超分辨率任务采用两种设置：各坐标轴下采样2倍（32 × 32 → 16 × 16），使用最近邻下采样；或下采样4倍（32 × 32 → 8 × 8），使用双三次下采样。图像归一化到 [0, 1]，并在此范围内加入标准差 σ_y ∈ {0.01, 0.05, 0.1} 的高斯测量噪声。作者的方法无需超参数；DPS 的尺度超参数则通过在128张验证图像上优化 LPIPS、MSE、PSNR 和 SSIM 选取（示例见图9）。作者发现，每个采样步骤都将去噪图像估计裁剪到 [0, 1] 的静态阈值处理，对于 DPS-D 和 ΠGDM-D 的稳定性与性能至关重要。Chung et al. (2022a) 曾将稳定性列为局限，并指出研究如何稳定采样器是一个有前景的方向。作者发现，TMPD-D 及其对角近似 DTMPD-D 在不同 SDE、噪声水平和观测映射下均保持稳定，无需静态阈值处理。对于 VE ΠGDM-D，作者发现 Song et al. (2023) 的原始算法稳定；对于 VP ΠGDM-D，原始算法在 FFHQ 上稳定，但在 CIFAR-10 上即使采用静态阈值处理仍不稳定。为使 ΠGDM-D 更接近本文算法，作者将其似然得分替换进祖先采样（Ancestral sampling）算法，而不是 Song et al. (2023) 建议的 DDIM 算法；这一做法生成了稳定样本。

<span id="S100"></span>
### S100 — paragraph
**Source:** pp.10–11 S100
**Original:** The methods TMPD, ΠGDM and DPS all have the same numerical solver of their respective reverse-SDE, and DTMPD-D, ΠGDM-D and DPS-D all use DDPM since DDIM and DDPM are equivalent algorithms with our chosen DDIM hyperparameter η = 1.0. Therefore, the sampling methods being compared only differ in the ∇_{x_t} log p_{y|t}(y|x_t) term in their reverse-SDE, and so this experiment allows us to study the behaviour of our method on inpainting and super-resolution compared to the different approximations of the smoothed likelihood. A summary of the results for CIFAR-10 sampled using VE DDPM are shown in Table 2. The complete results are in Tables 8 and 12 for VP and VE DDPM respectively, and Tables 9 and 13 for score-based VP and VE-SDE, respectively. For more experimental details including illustration of samples used to generate the tables can be found in Appendix E.4. Our method is the only method able to provide high-quality reconstructions independently of the SDE, time discretization or noise level used. On the other hand, we see that DPS-D and ΠGDM-D are not able to provide high-quality reconstructions for the VE-SDE. For the continuous time methods, ΠGDM and DPS are outperformed by TMPD for both VE and VP-SDEs in the majority of tasks.  
**中文：** TMPD、ΠGDM 和 DPS 均使用各自反向 SDE 的同一种数值求解器；DTMPD-D、ΠGDM-D 和 DPS-D 均使用 DDPM，因为在本文选取的 DDIM 超参数 η = 1.0 下，DDIM 与 DDPM 等价。因此，所比较的采样方法仅在其反向 SDE 中的 ∇_{x_t} log p_{y|t}(y|x_t) 项不同，这使得该实验能够比较本文方法与不同平滑似然近似方法在图像修复和超分辨率任务上的表现。采用 VE DDPM 生成的 CIFAR-10 结果汇总于表2。VP 和 VE DDPM 的完整结果分别见表8和表12；基于 score 的 VP 和 VE-SDE 结果分别见表9和表13。更多实验细节（包括生成表格所用样本的示例图）见附录 E.4。本文方法是唯一能够在所用 SDE、时间离散方案或噪声水平变化时仍独立生成高质量重建结果的方法。另一方面，DPS-D 和 ΠGDM-D 在 VE-SDE 下无法生成高质量重建结果。对于连续时间方法，在大多数任务中，TMPD 在 VE 和 VP-SDE 下均优于 ΠGDM 和 DPS。

<span id="S101"></span>
### S101 — heading
**Source:** p.11 S101

**Original:** 7 Discussions, limitations and future work  
**中文：** 7 讨论、局限与未来工作

<span id="S102"></span>
### S102 — paragraph
**Source:** p.11 S102
**Original:** In this paper, we introduced TMPD, a diffusion modelling approach to solve inverse problems and sample from conditional distributions using unconditional diffusion models. On various tasks on the VP-SDE, TMPD achieves competitive quality with other methods that aim to solve the noisy, linear inverse problem while avoiding the expensive, problem-specific training of conditional models. Our method is more versatile since it can also be used for the VE-SDE, and for large noise and different time discretizations.  
**中文：** 本文提出 TMPD，这是一种利用无条件扩散模型求解逆问题并从条件分布采样的扩散建模方法。在 VP-SDE 的多种任务上，TMPD 在求解含噪线性逆问题时达到与其他方法相当的质量，同时避免了针对特定问题训练条件模型的高昂代价。该方法适用范围更广，还可用于 VE-SDE、大噪声以及不同的时间离散方案。

<span id="S103"></span>
### S103 — paragraph
**Source:** p.11 S103
**Original:** TMPD is slower, as each iteration costs more memory and compute due to the Jacobian over the score model. Even with a diagonal and row-sum approximation to the Jacobian, the method is around 1.5× slower than DPS and ΠGDM. The row-sum approximation is not suitable for inverse problems with more complicated, non-diagonal and nonlinear observation operators, therefore, it would be helpful to explore methods to circumvent heuristics. For example, heuristics can be circumvented by noting in the definition of Equation (11) does not require the inverse but rather solving a system of linear equations with right hand side y−Hm_{0|t}(x_t). A very recent work (Rozet et al., 2024), uses the natural choice of the conjugate gradient (CG) method to solve this linear system with success on non-diagonal and nonlinear observation operators, even for a small number of iterations of the CG method.  
**中文：** TMPD 的速度较慢，因为每次迭代都要计算 score 模型的 Jacobian，因而需要更多内存和计算量。即使对 Jacobian 采用对角近似和行和近似，该方法仍比 DPS 和 ΠGDM 慢约1.5倍。行和近似不适用于观测算子更复杂、非对角且非线性的逆问题，因此有必要探索能够避免启发式近似的方法。例如，注意到式（11）的定义并不要求显式求逆，而是可以求解右端项为 y−Hm_{0|t}(x_t) 的线性方程组，从而避免这类启发式处理。Rozet et al. (2024) 的一项近期工作采用自然选择的共轭梯度（conjugate gradient，CG）方法求解该线性系统；即使 CG 仅迭代少数几次，也能成功处理非对角、非线性观测算子。

<span id="S104"></span>
### S104 — paragraph
**Source:** p.11 S104
**Original:** Using flow based methods (Albergo & Vanden-Eijnden, 2022; Lipman et al., 2022) as unconditional priors and building conditional sampling methods that leverage flows as pretrained models (Ben-Hamu et al., 2024; Pandey et al., 2024) is related to the approach used in this paper. For example, it is possible to directly substitute in the TMPD approximation to the PiGDM method applied to flows (Pokle et al., 2023), which would be a fruitful direction for future work.  
**中文：** 将基于流（flow-based）的方法（Albergo & Vanden-Eijnden, 2022；Lipman et al., 2022）用作无条件先验，并构建利用预训练流模型的条件采样方法（Ben-Hamu et al., 2024；Pandey et al., 2024），与本文采用的思路相关。例如，可以将 TMPD 近似直接替换到应用于流模型的 PiGDM 方法中（Pokle et al., 2023）；这将是一个有价值的未来研究方向。

## Page 12

<span id="page-12"></span>
**Page status:** Complete

<span id="S105"></span>
### S105 — paragraph
**Source:** p.12 S105
**Original:** Many state of the art diffusion models operate in a latent space which would make any observation operator nonlinear, and thus linear observations maps do not suffice in the latent diffusion setting. Furthermore, for inverse problems in general it may be excessive to train a diffusion model for the full data distribution, and it could be difficult to outperform a diffusion model trained for a specific inverse problem. For example, using diffusion models trained specifically for the super-resolution problem in a cascade together with noise conditioning augmentation (Ho et al., 2022) has been extremely effective in progressively generating high-fidelity images, see e.g., Saharia et al. (2022).  
**中文：** 许多先进扩散模型在潜空间中运行，这会使任何观测算子都变成非线性，因此线性观测映射不足以适用于潜空间扩散设置。此外，对于一般逆问题，可能没有必要为完整数据分布训练扩散模型，而且也可能难以胜过针对某个特定逆问题训练的扩散模型。例如，将专门针对超分辨率训练的扩散模型级联使用，并结合噪声条件增强（Ho et al., 2022），已被证明能够有效地逐步生成高保真图像，例如 Saharia et al. (2022)。

<span id="S106"></span>
### S106 — paragraph
**Source:** p.12 S106
**Original:** A limitation of our method is that without approximations, the full method may be costly to implement in high dimensions of image data. On the positive side, our approach does not require any hyperparameter tuning, does not require static-thresholding of the denoised image, and is more principled when compared to existing approaches, as shown in Section 4. We show that our method, unlike ΠGDM, does not fail for the cases where the additive Gaussian noise is significant. We provided a way to analyse similar methods and our moment approximations can be analysed more rigorously to provide deeper theoretical results for these kinds of methods. Our future work plans include expanding the analysis we provided in this work.  
**中文：** 本文方法的一项局限是：若不作近似，在图像数据的高维场景中实现完整方法可能代价较高。另一方面，与现有方法相比（见第4节），本文方法无需调节超参数，也无需对去噪图像进行静态阈值处理，且具有更坚实的理论依据。作者表明，与 ΠGDM 不同，当加性高斯噪声较大时，本文方法不会失效。本文还提供了一种分析同类方法的途径；对本文的矩近似进行更严格的分析，有望为这类方法带来更深入的理论结果。未来工作计划扩展本文给出的分析。

**中文：** 其中，\(J_\lambda\) 是 \(\lambda\) 关于 \(x_t\) 的 Jacobian，\(S_\lambda\) 是 \(\lambda\) 关于 \(x_t\) 的 Hessian。再回忆 \(\eta_0=x_0\sqrt{\alpha_t}/v_t\)，并且：

## Page 17

<span id="page-17"></span>

<span id="S203"></span>
### S203 — heading
**Source:** p.17 S203
**Original:** B.2 Proof of Theorem 1  
**中文：** B.2 定理1的证明

<span id="S204"></span>
### S204 — paragraph
**Source:** p.17 S204
**Original:** We first introduce three SDEs.  
**中文：** 首先介绍三个随机微分方程（SDE）。

<span id="S205"></span>
### S205 — heading
**Source:** p.17 S205
**Original:** Conditioned SDEs: The first pair of SDEs is given by an OU process, but started in the conditional distribution:  
**中文：** 条件 SDE：第一对 SDE 由 Ornstein–Uhlenbeck（OU）过程给出，但其初始分布取条件分布：

<span id="S206"></span>
### S206 — equation
**Source:** p.17 S206
**Original:** \(dx_t^c=-\frac{1}{2}x_t^c\,dt+dw_t,\qquad x_0^c\sim p_0^c:=p_{\mathrm{data}}(x_0|y).\tag{18}\)  
**中文：** \(dx_t^c=-\frac{1}{2}x_t^c\,dt+dw_t,\qquad x_0^c\sim p_0^c:=p_{\mathrm{data}}(x_0|y).\tag{18}\)

<span id="S207"></span>
### S207 — paragraph
**Source:** p.17 S207
**Original:** We denote the marginals of \(x_t^c\) by \(p_t^c\) to differentiate them from the marginals \(p_t\) of the OU process started in the correct distribution, defined in Equation (2). Note that \(p_t^c=p_t(x_t|y)\). The time reversal \(z_t=x_{T-t}\) then satisfies the SDE  
**中文：** 将 \(x_t^c\) 的边缘分布记为 \(p_t^c\)，以区别于从正确分布出发的 OU 过程在式（2）中定义的边缘分布 \(p_t\)。注意 \(p_t^c=p_t(x_t|y)\)。时间反演 \(z_t=x_{T-t}\) 满足以下 SDE：

<span id="S208"></span>
### S208 — equation
**Source:** p.17 S208
**Original:** \(\begin{aligned}dz_t&=\frac{1}{2}z_t\,dt+\nabla_{z_t}\log p^c_{T-t}(z_t)\,dt+dw_t\\&=\frac{1}{2}z_t\,dt+\nabla_{z_t}\log p_{T-t}(z_t)\,dt+\nabla_{z_t}\log p_{y|T-t}(y|z_t)\,dt+dw_t,\\z_0&\sim p_T^c.\end{aligned}\tag{19}\)  
**中文：** \(\begin{aligned}dz_t&=\frac{1}{2}z_t\,dt+\nabla_{z_t}\log p^c_{T-t}(z_t)\,dt+dw_t\\&=\frac{1}{2}z_t\,dt+\nabla_{z_t}\log p_{T-t}(z_t)\,dt+\nabla_{z_t}\log p_{y|T-t}(y|z_t)\,dt+dw_t,\\z_0&\sim p_T^c.\end{aligned}\tag{19}\)

<span id="S209"></span>
### S209 — paragraph
**Source:** p.17 S209
**Original:** where we used Equation (3). Solutions to the above reverse SDE will sample our target measure p_0^c=p_data(x_0|y) at final time. Therefore, we want to study how our algorithm approximates solutions of x^c.  
**中文：** 其中使用了式（3）。上述反向 SDE 的解在终止时刻将从目标测度 \(p_0^c=p_{\mathrm{data}}(x_0|y)\) 采样。因此，作者希望研究算法对 \(x^c\) 的解近似得如何。

<span id="S210"></span>
### S210 — heading
**Source:** p.17 S210
**Original:** Intermediate Gaussian SDE:  
**中文：** 中间高斯 SDE：

<span id="S211"></span>
### S211 — paragraph
**Source:** p.17 S211
**Original:** Instead of bounding the distance of solutions to the above conditioned reverse SDE to our algorithm, we will instead introduce an intermediate process, which we will later use in a triangle inequality. The process will be a Gaussian process. Therefore, we denote it with a superscript G.  
**中文：** 作者不直接界定上述条件反向 SDE 的解与算法输出之间的距离，而是引入一个中间过程，之后用于三角不等式。该过程为高斯过程，因此用上标 G 表示。

<span id="S212"></span>
### S212 — paragraph
**Source:** p.17 S212
**Original:** The process \(x_t^G\) will be defined analogous to Equation (2), but assuming that it is started in \(\mathcal{N}(x_0;\mu_0,\Sigma_0)\) instead of \(p_{\mathrm{data}}\). Since the forward SDE in Equation (2) is linear, all of the marginals of \(x_t^G\), which we denote by \(p_t^G\), will also be Gaussian.  
**中文：** 过程 \(x_t^G\) 的定义与式（2）类似，但假设它从 \(\mathcal{N}(x_0;\mu_0,\Sigma_0)\) 而非 \(p_{\mathrm{data}}\) 出发。由于式（2）的前向 SDE 是线性的，\(x_t^G\) 的所有边缘分布也都是高斯分布，记为 \(p_t^G\)。

<span id="S213"></span>
### S213 — paragraph
**Source:** p.17 S213
**Original:** Again, we define a conditioned version of x, called \(x^{G,c}\) analogous to Equation (18), i.e. \(x_0^{G,c}\) will be distributed as \(x_0^G\) conditioned on \(y=y\), i.e. \(p^G(x_0|y=y)\). Since we have a linear observation model, \(p^G(x_0|y=y)\) is still a Gaussian, and therefore \(x^{G,c}\) will also be a Gaussian process. Its reverse SDE \(z^{G,c}\) is defined through Equation (19), just that every appearance of p will also have a superscript G,  
**中文：** 同样，定义 \(x\) 的条件版本 \(x^{G,c}\)，记法与式（18）类似：\(x_0^{G,c}\) 服从在 \(y=y\) 条件下的 \(x_0^G\) 分布，即 \(p^G(x_0|y=y)\)。由于观测模型是线性的，\(p^G(x_0|y=y)\) 仍为高斯分布，因此 \(x^{G,c}\) 也是高斯过程。其反向 SDE \(z^{G,c}\) 按式（19）定义，只需将其中每个 \(p\) 都加上上标 G：

<span id="S214"></span>
### S214 — equation
**Source:** p.17 S214
**Original:** \(dz_t=\frac{1}{2}z_t\,dt+\nabla_{z_t}\log p^G_{T-t}(z_t)\,dt+\nabla_{z_t}\log p^G_{T-t}(y|z_t)\,dt+dw_t,\qquad z_0\sim p_T^c.\tag{20}\)  
**中文：** \(dz_t=\frac{1}{2}z_t\,dt+\nabla_{z_t}\log p^G_{T-t}(z_t)\,dt+\nabla_{z_t}\log p^G_{T-t}(y|z_t)\,dt+dw_t,\qquad z_0\sim p_T^c.\tag{20}\)

<span id="S215"></span>
### S215 — heading
**Source:** p.17 S215
**Original:** Algorithm SDE:  
**中文：** 算法 SDE：

<span id="S216"></span>
### S216 — paragraph
**Source:** p.17 S216
**Original:** Finally, we define a third reverse SDE, which is the SDE that we are discretizing when implementing our algorithm. It is given by  
**中文：** 最后定义第三个反向 SDE，即实现算法时进行离散化的 SDE：

<span id="S217"></span>
### S217 — equation
**Source:** p.17 S217
**Original:** \(dz_t=\frac{1}{2}z_t\,dt+\nabla_{z_t}\log p_{T-t}(z_t)\,dt+f_{T-t}(z_t)\,dt+dw_t,\qquad z_0\sim p_T^c.\tag{21}\)  
**中文：** \(dz_t=\frac{1}{2}z_t\,dt+\nabla_{z_t}\log p_{T-t}(z_t)\,dt+f_{T-t}(z_t)\,dt+dw_t,\qquad z_0\sim p_T^c.\tag{21}\)

<span id="S218"></span>
### S218 — paragraph
**Source:** p.17 S218
**Original:** where  
**中文：** 其中

<span id="S219"></span>
### S219 — equation
**Source:** p.17 S219
**Original:** \(f_t(z_t)=\nabla_{z_t}\mathbb{E}[x_0|x_t=z_t]H^\top\left(H\mathbb{V}[x_0|x_t=z_t]H^\top+\sigma_y^2 I\right)^{-1}\left(y-H\mathbb{E}[x_0|x_t=z_t]\right),\tag{22}\)  
**中文：** \(f_t(z_t)=\nabla_{z_t}\mathbb{E}[x_0|x_t=z_t]H^\top\left(H\mathbb{V}[x_0|x_t=z_t]H^\top+\sigma_y^2 I\right)^{-1}\left(y-H\mathbb{E}[x_0|x_t=z_t]\right),\tag{22}\)

<span id="S220"></span>
### S220 — paragraph
**Source:** p.17 S220
**Original:** and V denotes the conditional covariance. Except for approximation errors due to time discretization and the initial conditions, this is the SDE we are sampling from in our algorithm. We denote the marginal of z_t by q_t. Now we are ready to write our proof.  
**中文：** 其中 \(\mathbb{V}\) 表示条件协方差。除时间离散化和初始条件造成的近似误差外，这就是算法实际采样的 SDE。将 \(z_t\) 的边缘分布记为 \(q_t\)。现在开始证明。

<span id="S221"></span>
### S221 — paragraph
**Source:** p.17 S221
**Original:** Proof of Theorem 1. We have that p_data(·|y)=p_0^c  
**中文：** 定理1的证明。由定义，\(p_{\mathrm{data}}(\cdot|y)=p_0^c\)。

<span id="S222"></span>
### S222 — equation
**Source:** p.17 S222
**Original:** \(\|p_0^c-q_T\|_{\mathrm{TV}}\leq\|p_0^c-p_0^{G,c}\|_{\mathrm{TV}}+\|p_0^{G,c}-q_T\|_{\mathrm{TV}}.\)  
**中文：** \(\|p_0^c-q_T\|_{\mathrm{TV}}\leq\|p_0^c-p_0^{G,c}\|_{\mathrm{TV}}+\|p_0^{G,c}-q_T\|_{\mathrm{TV}}.\)

<span id="S223"></span>
### S223 — paragraph
**Source:** p.17 S223
**Original:** We will bound the second term using Pinsker’s inequality to get a KL term on the path space, as done in Chen et al. (2023). The proof then consists of bounding the first total variation term and the resulting KL term.  
**中文：** 按照 Chen et al. (2023) 的做法，使用 Pinsker 不等式将第二项转化为路径空间上的 KL 项。随后分别界定第一项全变差距离和得到的 KL 项。

## Page 18

<span id="page-18"></span>
**Page status:** Complete

<span id="S224"></span>
### S224 — heading
**Source:** p.18 S224
**Original:** Bounding the first term: The first term is the total variation distance between p_0(x_0|y=y) and p_0^{G}(x_0^{G}|y=y). Now,  
**中文：** 界定第一项：第一项是 \(p_0(x_0|y=y)\) 与 \(p_0^{G}(x_0^{G}|y=y)\) 之间的全变差距离。现在，

<span id="S225"></span>
### S225 — equation
**Source:** p.18 S225
**Original:** \(\begin{aligned}p_0(x_0=x_0|y=y)&=\frac{p_{y|0}(y=y|x_0=x_0)p_0(x_0=x_0)}{p_y(y=y)}\\&=\frac{p^G_{y|0}(y=y|x_0=x_0)p_0^G(x_0=x_0)}{p_y^G(y=y)}\frac{p_0(x_0=x_0)}{p_0^G(x_0^G=x_0^G)}\frac{p_y^G(y=y)}{p_y(y=y)}\\&=p^G_{y|0}(x_0=x_0|y=y)\exp(\Phi(x_0))\frac{p_y^G(y=y)}{p_y(y=y)}.\end{aligned}\)  
**中文：** \(\begin{aligned}p_0(x_0=x_0|y=y)&=\frac{p_{y|0}(y=y|x_0=x_0)p_0(x_0=x_0)}{p_y(y=y)}\\&=\frac{p^G_{y|0}(y=y|x_0=x_0)p_0^G(x_0=x_0)}{p_y^G(y=y)}\frac{p_0(x_0=x_0)}{p_0^G(x_0^G=x_0^G)}\frac{p_y^G(y=y)}{p_y(y=y)}\\&=p^G_{y|0}(x_0=x_0|y=y)\exp(\Phi(x_0))\frac{p_y^G(y=y)}{p_y(y=y)}.\end{aligned}\)

<span id="S226"></span>
### S226 — paragraph
**Source:** p.18 S226
**Original:** where we used that the conditional distribution of y given x_0 does not depend on the distribution of x_0. For the last term we get  
**中文：** 这里利用了给定 \(x_0\) 时 \(y\) 的条件分布不依赖于 \(x_0\) 的边缘分布。对于最后一项，有：

<span id="S227"></span>
### S227 — equation
**Source:** p.18 S227
**Original:** \(\begin{aligned}p_y(y=y)&=\int p_{y|0}(y=y|x_0=x_0)p_0(x_0=x_0)\,dx_0\\&=\int p^G_{y|0}(y=y|x_0=x_0)p_0^G(x_0=x_0)\exp(\Phi(x_0))\,dx_0\\&=Np_y^G(y=y),\end{aligned}\)  
**中文：** \(\begin{aligned}p_y(y=y)&=\int p_{y|0}(y=y|x_0=x_0)p_0(x_0=x_0)\,dx_0\\&=\int p^G_{y|0}(y=y|x_0=x_0)p_0^G(x_0=x_0)\exp(\Phi(x_0))\,dx_0\\&=Np_y^G(y=y),\end{aligned}\)

**中文：** 其中 \(N\in[1/M,M]\)。因此，

<span id="S231"></span>
### S231 — heading
**Source:** p.18 S231
**Original:** Bounding the second term:  
**中文：** 界定第二项：

<span id="S232"></span>
### S232 — paragraph
**Source:** p.18 S232
**Original:** For the second term we get that \(p_0^{G,c}\) is the final marginal of Equation (20), while \(q_T\) is the final marginal of Equation (21). We now use Pinsker’s inequality,  
**中文：** 对于第二项，\(p_0^{G,c}\) 是式（20）的终止边缘分布，\(q_T\) 是式（21）的终止边缘分布。现在使用 Pinsker 不等式：

<span id="S233"></span>
### S233 — equation
**Source:** p.18 S233
**Original:** \(\|p_0^{G,c}-q_T\|_{\mathrm{TV}}\leq\sqrt{\mathrm{KL}(p_0^{G,c}\|q_T)}.\)  
**中文：** \(\|p_0^{G,c}-q_T\|_{\mathrm{TV}}\leq\sqrt{\mathrm{KL}(p_0^{G,c}\|q_T)}.\)

<span id="S234"></span>
### S234 — paragraph
**Source:** p.18 S234
**Original:** We denote the path measures induced by Equation (20) and Equation (21) by \(\mathbb{P}^{G,c}\) and \(\mathbb{Q}\) respectively. They have final time marginals \(p_0^{G,c}\) and \(q_T\). Therefore, we can bound the KL-Divergence of the marginals \(p_0^{G,c}\) and \(q_T\) by the KL-Divergence on the full path space:  
**中文：** 将式（20）和式（21）诱导的路径测度分别记为 \(\mathbb{P}^{G,c}\) 和 \(\mathbb{Q}\)。它们在终止时刻的边缘分布分别为 \(p_0^{G,c}\) 和 \(q_T\)。因此，可以用完整路径空间上的 KL 散度界定边缘分布 \(p_0^{G,c}\) 与 \(q_T\) 之间的 KL 散度：

<span id="S235"></span>
### S235 — equation
**Source:** p.18 S235
**Original:** \(\mathrm{KL}(p_0^{G,c}\|q_T)\leq\mathrm{KL}(\mathbb{P}^{G,c}\|\mathbb{Q}).\)  
**中文：** \(\mathrm{KL}(p_0^{G,c}\|q_T)\leq\mathrm{KL}(\mathbb{P}^{G,c}\|\mathbb{Q}).\)

<span id="S236"></span>
### S236 — paragraph
**Source:** p.18 S236
**Original:** We will assume that we can apply Girsanov’s theorem and show later that this is justified. Using Girsanov’s theorem, we can evaluate the Radon-Nikodym derivative \(\frac{d\mathbb{Q}}{d\mathbb{P}^{G,c}}\) on the path space and therefore calculate the KL-divergence:  
**中文：** 先假设可以应用 Girsanov 定理，稍后再证明该假设成立。利用 Girsanov 定理，可以在路径空间上求出 Radon–Nikodym 导数 \(\frac{d\mathbb{Q}}{d\mathbb{P}^{G,c}}\)，从而计算 KL 散度：

<span id="S237"></span>
### S237 — equation
**Source:** p.18 S237
**Original:** \(\begin{aligned}\mathrm{KL}(\mathbb{P}^{G,c}\|\mathbb{Q})&=\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}^{G,c}}\left[\log\frac{d\mathbb{P}^{G,c}}{d\mathbb{Q}}\right]\\&=\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}^{G,c}}\left[-\int_0^t\left(\nabla\log p_t(x_t)+f_{T-t}(x_t)-\nabla\log p^{G,c}_{T-t}(x_t)\right)dw_t\right]\\&\quad+\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}^{G,c}}\left[\int_0^t\left\|\nabla\log p_t(x_t)+f_{T-t}(x_t)-\nabla\log p^{G,c}_{T-t}(x_t)\right\|^2dt\right]\\&=\int_0^t\mathbb{E}_{p_t^{G,c}}\left[\left\|\nabla\log p_t(x_t)+f_{T-t}(x_t)-\nabla\log p^{G,c}_{T-t}(x_t)\right\|^2\right]dt.\end{aligned}\)  
**中文：** \(\begin{aligned}\mathrm{KL}(\mathbb{P}^{G,c}\|\mathbb{Q})&=\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}^{G,c}}\left[\log\frac{d\mathbb{P}^{G,c}}{d\mathbb{Q}}\right]\\&=\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}^{G,c}}\left[-\int_0^t\left(\nabla\log p_t(x_t)+f_{T-t}(x_t)-\nabla\log p^{G,c}_{T-t}(x_t)\right)dw_t\right]\\&\quad+\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}^{G,c}}\left[\int_0^t\left\|\nabla\log p_t(x_t)+f_{T-t}(x_t)-\nabla\log p^{G,c}_{T-t}(x_t)\right\|^2dt\right]\\&=\int_0^t\mathbb{E}_{p_t^{G,c}}\left[\left\|\nabla\log p_t(x_t)+f_{T-t}(x_t)-\nabla\log p^{G,c}_{T-t}(x_t)\right\|^2\right]dt.\end{aligned}\)

**Original:** \(\begin{aligned}\nabla_{x_t}\int x_0p(x_0|x_t)\,dx_0&=\int x_0\nabla_{x_t}p^G(x_0|x_t)\exp(\Phi(x_0)-\Phi_t(x_t))\,dx_0\\&=\int x_0\exp(\Phi(x_0)-\Phi_t(x_t))\nabla_{x_t}p^G(x_0|x_t)\,dx_0\\&\quad+\int x_0\nabla_{x_t}\Phi_t(x_t)\exp(\Phi(x_0)-\Phi_t(x_t))p^G(x_0|x_t)\,dx_0\\&=\int x_0\exp(\Phi(x_0)-\Phi_t(x_t))\nabla_{x_t}p^G(x_0|x_t)\,dx_0+\nabla_{x_t}\Phi_t(x_t)\mathbb{E}[x_0|x_t=x_t].\end{aligned}\tag{31}\)  
**中文：** \(\begin{aligned}\nabla_{x_t}\int x_0p(x_0|x_t)\,dx_0&=\int x_0\nabla_{x_t}p^G(x_0|x_t)\exp(\Phi(x_0)-\Phi_t(x_t))\,dx_0\\&=\int x_0\exp(\Phi(x_0)-\Phi_t(x_t))\nabla_{x_t}p^G(x_0|x_t)\,dx_0\\&\quad+\int x_0\nabla_{x_t}\Phi_t(x_t)\exp(\Phi(x_0)-\Phi_t(x_t))p^G(x_0|x_t)\,dx_0\\&=\int x_0\exp(\Phi(x_0)-\Phi_t(x_t))\nabla_{x_t}p^G(x_0|x_t)\,dx_0+\nabla_{x_t}\Phi_t(x_t)\mathbb{E}[x_0|x_t=x_t].\end{aligned}\tag{31}\)

### S275 — equation
**Source:** p.21 S275
**Original:** \(\begin{aligned}&\left\|\int x_0\exp(\Phi(x_0)-\Phi_t(x_t))\nabla_{x_t}p^G(x_0|x_t)\,dx_0-\nabla_{x_t}\mathbb{E}[x_0^G|x_t^G=x_t]\right\|\\&=\left\|\int\nabla_{x_t}p^G(x_0|x_t)x_0^\top(\exp(\Phi(x_0)-\Phi_t(x_t))-1)\,dx_0\right\|\\&\leq(M^2-1)\int p^G(x_0|x_t)\|\nabla\log p^G(x_0|x_t)x_0^\top\|\,dx_0\\&\leq(M^2-1)\left(\int p^G(x_0|x_t)\|\nabla\log p^G(x_0|x_t)x_0^\top\|\,dx_0\right)^{1/2}\\&\leq(M^2-1)\left(\int p^G(x_0|x_t)x_0^\top\Sigma^{-1}_{0|t}(x_0-m^{x_t}_{0|t})\,dx_0\right)^{1/2}\\&\lesssim(M^2-1)\left(\int p^G(x_0|x_t)(x_0-m^{x_t}_{0|t})^\top\Sigma^{-1}_{0|t}(x_0-m^{x_t}_{0|t})\,dx_0\right)^{1/2}\\&\lesssim\|x_t\|(M^2-1)\sqrt{d_x}\\&\lesssim\|x_t\|(M^2-1).\end{aligned}\tag{32}\)  
**中文：** \(\begin{aligned}&\left\|\int x_0\exp(\Phi(x_0)-\Phi_t(x_t))\nabla_{x_t}p^G(x_0|x_t)\,dx_0-\nabla_{x_t}\mathbb{E}[x_0^G|x_t^G=x_t]\right\|\\&=\left\|\int\nabla_{x_t}p^G(x_0|x_t)x_0^\top(\exp(\Phi(x_0)-\Phi_t(x_t))-1)\,dx_0\right\|\\&\leq(M^2-1)\int p^G(x_0|x_t)\|\nabla\log p^G(x_0|x_t)x_0^\top\|\,dx_0\\&\leq(M^2-1)\left(\int p^G(x_0|x_t)\|\nabla\log p^G(x_0|x_t)x_0^\top\|\,dx_0\right)^{1/2}\\&\leq(M^2-1)\left(\int p^G(x_0|x_t)x_0^\top\Sigma^{-1}_{0|t}(x_0-m^{x_t}_{0|t})\,dx_0\right)^{1/2}\\&\lesssim(M^2-1)\left(\int p^G(x_0|x_t)(x_0-m^{x_t}_{0|t})^\top\Sigma^{-1}_{0|t}(x_0-m^{x_t}_{0|t})\,dx_0\right)^{1/2}\\&\lesssim\|x_t\|(M^2-1)\sqrt{d_x}\\&\lesssim\|x_t\|(M^2-1).\end{aligned}\tag{32}\)
