### S276 — paragraph
**Source:** p.21 S276
**Original:** Here we used that we can upper bound the operator norm of a positive semidefinite matrix by its trace from the third to the fourth line. We denoted by \(m^{x_t}_{0|t}\) and \(\Sigma_{0|t}\) the mean and covariance of \(p^G(x_0^G|x_t^G=x_t)\). From the second-to-last to the last line we used that \(m^{x_t}_{0|t}\) depends on \(x_t\) linearly, and the magnitude of the linear dependence can be bounded uniformly in \(t\) (see Equation (44)). The integral in the last line is the variance of a standard normal random variable, which evaluates to \(d_x\). Furthermore,  
**中文：** 这里在从第三行到第四行时，利用了半正定矩阵的算子范数可由其迹上界这一事实。将 \(m^{x_t}_{0|t}\) 和 \(\Sigma_{0|t}\) 分别记为 \(p^G(x_0^G|x_t^G=x_t)\) 的均值和协方差。从倒数第二行到最后一行，利用了 \(m^{x_t}_{0|t}\) 关于 \(x_t\) 呈线性依赖，且该线性依赖的幅度可对所有 \(t\) 一致有界（见式（44））。最后一行的积分是标准正态随机变量的方差，结果为 \(d_x\)。此外，

<span id="S290"></span>
### S290 — equation
**Source:** p.22 S290
**Original:** \(\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}^{G,c}}\left[\exp\left(\frac{1}{2}\int_{t_i}^{t_{i+1}}\|\nabla\log p_t(x_t)+f_t(x_t)-\nabla\log p_t^{G,c}(x_t)\|^2dt\right)\right]\leq\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}^{G,c}}\left[\exp\left(c\int_{t_i}^{t_{i+1}}(1+\|x_t\|^2)dt\right)\right].\)  
**中文：** \(\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}^{G,c}}\left[\exp\left(\frac{1}{2}\int_{t_i}^{t_{i+1}}\|\nabla\log p_t(x_t)+f_t(x_t)-\nabla\log p_t^{G,c}(x_t)\|^2dt\right)\right]\leq\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}^{G,c}}\left[\exp\left(c\int_{t_i}^{t_{i+1}}(1+\|x_t\|^2)dt\right)\right].\)

<span id="S291"></span>
### S291 — paragraph
**Source:** p.22 S291
**Original:** Since the expectation is regarding a Gaussian random variable \(x_t\), we can make it finite as long as we pick \(\Delta_i=t_{i+1}-t_i\) small enough. By setting \(t_0=0\) and \(t_1>0\), we can show equivalence on \([t_0,t_1]\). We can then iterate this procedure, to get equivalence on \([t_1,t_2]\) and so on. Since the lowest and highest eigenvalues of \(\Sigma_t\) are bounded from below and above respectively, and \(m_t\) is also bounded, the \(\Delta_i\) can be bounded from below. Therefore, we get equivalence on \([0,T]\) this way in at most \(\lceil T/\Delta\rceil\) steps.  
**中文：** 由于期望是针对高斯随机变量 \(x_t\) 计算的，只要将 \(\Delta_i=t_{i+1}-t_i\) 取得足够小，就能使其有限。令 \(t_0=0\)、\(t_1>0\)，即可证明两个测度在 \([t_0,t_1]\) 上等价；随后迭代该过程，依次得到 \([t_1,t_2]\) 等区间上的等价性。由于 \(\Sigma_t\) 的最小和最大特征值分别有正的下界和有限的上界，且 \(m_t\) 有界，因此 \(\Delta_i\) 可以有统一的正下界。由此，至多经过 \(\lceil T/\Delta\rceil\) 步即可得到整个 \([0,T]\) 上的等价性。

### S335 — paragraph
**Source:** p.23 S335
**Original:** The matrix \(\frac{v_t}{\alpha_t}(I_{d_x}+v_t\nabla_{x_t}s_\theta(x_t,t))\) needs to be inverted to calculate the log likelihood, and therefore must be both symmetric and positive definite for all time, which puts the requirement that \(s_\theta(x_t,t)\) can be written as the negative gradient of a potential, however it is only approximated as such \(s_\theta(x_t,t)\approx\nabla\log p_{x_t}(x_t)\).  
**中文：** 为计算对数似然，需要对矩阵 \(\frac{v_t}{\alpha_t}(I_{d_x}+v_t\nabla_{x_t}s_\theta(x_t,t))\) 求逆，因此该矩阵必须在所有时刻均为对称正定矩阵。这要求 \(s_\theta(x_t,t)\) 能写成某个势函数的负梯度；然而实际中它只是近似如此，即 \(s_\theta(x_t,t)\approx\nabla\log p_{x_t}(x_t)\)。

<span id="S351"></span>
### S351 — paragraph
**Source:** p.24 S351
**Original:** The code for all of the experiments and instructions to run them are available at github.com/bb515/tmpdjax and github.com/bb515/tmpdtorch.  
**中文：** 所有实验代码及运行说明见 [github.com/bb515/tmpdjax](https://github.com/bb515/tmpdjax) 和 [github.com/bb515/tmpdtorch](https://github.com/bb515/tmpdtorch)。

<span id="S352"></span>
### S352 — algorithm
**Source:** p.24 S352
**Original:**
```text
Algorithm 1 TMPD-D (Ancestral sampling, VP)
input y, σ_y
x_N ∼ N(0, I_{d_x})
for n = N−1, ..., 0 do
    m_{0|t} ← 1/√α_n (x_n + v_n s_θ(x_n,t_n))
    m^y_{0|t} ← m_{0|t} + v_n/√α_n ∇_{x_n}m_{0|t} H^T (H v_n/√α_n ∇_{x_n}m_{0|t} H^T + σ_y² I_{d_y})^{-1}(y−Hm_{0|t})
    z_n ∼ N(0, I_{d_x})
    σ_n ← √((1−α_{n−1})β_n/(1−α_n))
    x_{n−1} ← √(1−β_n)(1−α_{n−1})/(1−α_n) x_n + √(α_{n−1}β_n)/(1−α_n)m^y_{0|t} + σ_n z_n
end for
output x_0
```
**中文：**
```text
算法1 TMPD-D（VP 的祖先采样）
输入 y、σ_y
x_N ∼ N(0, I_{d_x})
对 n = N−1, ..., 0 循环：
    m_{0|t} ← 1/√α_n (x_n + v_n s_θ(x_n,t_n))
    m^y_{0|t} ← m_{0|t} + v_n/√α_n ∇_{x_n}m_{0|t} H^T (H v_n/√α_n ∇_{x_n}m_{0|t} H^T + σ_y² I_{d_y})^{-1}(y−Hm_{0|t})
    z_n ∼ N(0, I_{d_x})
    σ_n ← √((1−α_{n−1})β_n/(1−α_n))
    x_{n−1} ← √(1−β_n)(1−α_{n−1})/(1−α_n) x_n + √(α_{n−1}β_n)/(1−α_n)m^y_{0|t} + σ_n z_n
循环结束
输出 x_0
```

<span id="S353"></span>
### S353 — heading
**Source:** p.24 S353
**Original:** E.1 Computational Complexity  
**中文：** E.1 计算复杂度

<span id="S354"></span>
### S354 — paragraph
**Source:** p.24 S354
**Original:** Let \(N\) is the number of noise scales, and let \(d_y\) is the dimensions of the observation, \(d_x\) are the dimension of the image and \(T_s\) is the time complexity of evaluating the score network. Computing a vector-Jacobian product has time complexity \(T_s\). Then, the time complexity of TMPD (not including fast matrix-vector products) is \(O(N(d_y^3+T_sd_y+T_s+T_s))\). ΠGDM comes at a smaller computational cost due to needing only \(O(1)\) vector-Jacobian-products, instead of \(O(d_y)\), resulting in a time complexity of \(O(N(T_s+T_s))\). DPS comes at the same complexity as ΠGDM.  
**中文：** 令 \(N\) 为噪声尺度数量，\(d_y\) 为观测维度，\(d_x\) 为图像维度，\(T_s\) 为计算 score 网络的时间复杂度。计算一次向量–Jacobian 积的时间复杂度为 \(T_s\)。因此，TMPD 的时间复杂度（不计快速矩阵–向量乘法）为 \(O(N(d_y^3+T_sd_y+T_s+T_s))\)。ΠGDM 只需 \(O(1)\) 次向量–Jacobian 积，而非 \(O(d_y)\) 次，因此计算代价较低，时间复杂度为 \(O(N(T_s+T_s))\)。DPS 的复杂度与 ΠGDM 相同。

<span id="S355"></span>
### S355 — paragraph
**Source:** pp.24–25 S355
**Original:** Whereas TMPD requires calculating the Jacobian which has memory complexity of at least \(O(d_xd_y)\). This is too large for high resolution image problems where the dimension of the observation is large. In comparison, the memory complexity of \(\Pi\)GDM depends on the observation operator, but for the class of problems that are explored in Song et al. (2023) is \(O(d_x)\). DPS comes at the same memory complexity as \(\Pi\)GDM.  
**中文：** 另一方面，TMPD 需要计算 Jacobian，其内存复杂度至少为 \(O(d_xd_y)\)。当观测维度较大时，这一开销对于高分辨率图像问题过高。相比之下， \(\Pi\)GDM 的内存复杂度取决于观测算子；但对于 Song et al. (2023) 所研究的问题类别，该复杂度为 \(O(d_x)\)。DPS 的内存复杂度与 \(\Pi\)GDM 相同。
## Page 25

<span id="page-25"></span>
**Page status:** Complete

<span id="S356"></span>
### S356 — paragraph
**Source:** p.25 S356
**Original:** As mentioned in the text, we make the following approximation  
**中文：** 如正文所述，采用下列近似：

<span id="S357"></span>
### S357 — equation
**Source:** p.25 S357
**Original:** \(D_{\mathrm{TMPD}}\nabla_{x_t}\log p_{y|t}(y|x_t)\approx\nabla_{x_t}m_{0|t}H^\top\left(H\frac{v_t}{\sqrt{\alpha_t}}\operatorname{diag}(\nabla_{x_t}m_{0|t})H^\top+\sigma_y^2I_{d_y}\right)^{-1}(y-Hm_{0|t}).\)  
**中文：** \(D_{\mathrm{TMPD}}\nabla_{x_t}\log p_{y|t}(y|x_t)\approx\nabla_{x_t}m_{0|t}H^\top\left(H\frac{v_t}{\sqrt{\alpha_t}}\operatorname{diag}(\nabla_{x_t}m_{0|t})H^\top+\sigma_y^2I_{d_y}\right)^{-1}(y-Hm_{0|t}).\)

<span id="S358"></span>
### S358 — paragraph
**Source:** p.25 S358
**Original:** Whilst this approximation does not require a linear solve, taking out the \(O(Nd_y^3)\) time complexity term, we would still like to take out the \(O(NT_s d_y)\) time complexity and \(O(d_xd_y)\) memory complexity term from calculating and storing the Jacobian, respectively, since this is too large for solving high resolution image applications. A further approximation approximates the diagonal of the Jacobian by the row sum of the Jacobian which only requires one vector-Jacobian product and brings the memory and time complexity of DTMPD down to that of \(\Pi\)GDM and DPS. We use this approximation in the image experiments and find that in practice it is only \((1.5\pm0.1)\times\) slower than \(\Pi\)GDM and DPS across all of our experiments. The row sum will be a good approximation of the diagonal when the Jacobian is approximately diagonal, which happens when there is small linear correlation between observation pixels, which we found to work well for super-resolution and inpainting.  
**中文：** 尽管这一近似不需要求解线性系统、因而去除了 \(O(Nd_y^3)\) 时间复杂度项，我们仍希望去除计算和存储 Jacobian 分别带来的 \(O(NT_s d_y)\) 时间复杂度项与 \(O(d_xd_y)\) 内存复杂度项，因为这些开销对于高分辨率图像问题过大。进一步地，可用 Jacobian 的行和近似其对角线；这只需一次向量－Jacobian 积，并可将 DTMPD 的内存与时间复杂度降至 \(\Pi\)GDM 和 DPS 的水平。图像实验采用了这一近似，结果发现，在全部实验中它的实际速度仅比 \(\Pi\)GDM 和 DPS 慢 \((1.5\pm0.1)\) 倍。当 Jacobian 近似为对角矩阵时，行和可以较好地近似其对角线；当观测像素间的线性相关性较弱时会出现这种情形，而作者发现该条件在超分辨率和图像修复任务中适用。

<span id="S359"></span>
### S359 — paragraph
**Source:** p.25 S359
**Original:** In inpainting, we have further improved the accuracy of the rowsum by instead of calculating the vector-Jacobian product evaluated at \(H\mathbf{1}\), masking out values of the vector in the vector-Jacobian product using the inpainting observation operator since they won’t contribute to the diagonal values of the variance.  
**中文：** 在图像修复任务中，作者还进一步提高了行和近似的精度：计算向量－Jacobian 积时，不再将其作用于 \(H\mathbf{1}\)，而是利用图像修复的观测算子屏蔽该向量中的相应分量，因为这些分量不会对方差矩阵对角线的取值产生贡献。

<span id="S360"></span>
### S360 — paragraph
**Source:** p.25 S360
**Original:** However, heuristics can be circumvented by noting in the definition of Equation (11) does not require the inverse but rather solving a system of linear equations with right hand side \(y-Hm_{0|t}(x_t)\). A very recent work (Rozet et al., 2024), uses the natural choice of the conjugate gradient (CG) method to solve this linear system noting that \(HC_{0|t}H^\top+\sigma_y^2I\) is symmetric positive definite (SPD) and is therefore compatible with the conjugate gradient (CG) method.  
**中文：** 不过，可以绕过这些启发式近似：注意到式（11）的定义并不要求显式求逆，而是要求求解右端项为 \(y-Hm_{0|t}(x_t)\) 的线性方程组。近期工作（Rozet et al., 2024）采用自然的选择——共轭梯度（conjugate gradient, CG）法——求解该线性系统；其依据是 \(HC_{0|t}H^\top+\sigma_y^2I\) 为对称正定（symmetric positive definite, SPD）矩阵，因此适用于 CG 法。

<span id="S361"></span>
### S361 — paragraph
**Source:** p.25 S361
**Original:** The CG method is an iterative algorithm to solve linear systems of the form \(Mv=b\) where SPD matrix \(M\) and vector \(b\) are known. Importantly, the CG method only requires implicit access to \(M\) through an operator that performs the matrix-vector product \(Mv\) given a vector \(v\). In our case, the linear system to solve is  
**中文：** CG 法是求解 \(Mv=b\) 形式线性系统的迭代算法，其中 SPD 矩阵 \(M\) 和向量 \(b\) 已知。关键在于，CG 法只需通过一个算子隐式访问 \(M\)：给定向量 \(v\)，该算子计算矩阵－向量积 \(Mv\)。在本文中，所要求解的线性系统为

<span id="S362"></span>
### S362 — equation
**Source:** p.25 S362
**Original:** \(y-Hm_{0|t}=(HC_{0|t}H^\top+\sigma_y^2I)v=H\frac{v_t}{\sqrt{\alpha_t}}\nabla_{x_t}m_{0|t}H^\top v+\sigma_y^2Iv.\)  
**中文：** \(y-Hm_{0|t}=(HC_{0|t}H^\top+\sigma_y^2I)v=H\frac{v_t}{\sqrt{\alpha_t}}\nabla_{x_t}m_{0|t}H^\top v+\sigma_y^2Iv.\)

<span id="S363"></span>
### S363 — paragraph
**Source:** p.25 S363
**Original:** The vector-Jacobian product \(v^\top H\nabla_{x_t}^\top m_{0|t}\) can be cheaply evaluated. In practice, there is no restriction on the score network to be the gradient of a potential, and the gradient of the score need not be SPD. Due to this and numerical errors, Rozet et al. (2024) observed that CG method becomes unstable after a large number of iterations and fails to reach an exact solution. Fortunately, the authors find that using very few iterations (1 to 3) of the CG method as part of the computation of the posterior score approximation leads to significant improvements over using heuristics for the covariance. Rozet et al. (2024) have successfully applied the CG method to non-sparse \(H\), such as accelerated MRI (where \(H\) is the composition of the Fourier transform and frequency subsampling).  
**中文：** 向量－Jacobian 积 \(v^\top H\nabla_{x_t}^\top m_{0|t}\) 可以低成本计算。实际中，score 网络不必是某个势函数的梯度，score 的梯度也不必为 SPD 矩阵。由于这一点以及数值误差，Rozet et al. (2024) 观察到，CG 迭代次数较多时会变得不稳定，并且无法达到精确解。幸运的是，作者发现，在后验 score 近似的计算中只运行很少的 CG 迭代（1 至 3 次），就能显著优于针对协方差使用启发式近似的结果。Rozet et al. (2024) 已成功将 CG 法用于非稀疏的 \(H\)，例如加速 MRI（此时 \(H\) 由傅里叶变换与频率子采样复合而成）。

<span id="S364"></span>
### S364 — heading
**Source:** p.25 S364
**Original:** E.2 Gaussian  
**中文：** E.2 高斯分布

<span id="S365"></span>
### S365 — paragraph
**Source:** p.25 S365
**Original:** When the data distribution \(p_0(x_0)\) is a (multivariate) Gaussian, then the reverse SDE is a linear SDE and we can calculate all of the terms needed to sample from the target posterior using diffusion explicitly. Moreover, we can sample from the target posterior using a direct or implicit method such as Cholesky decomposition. In this simple example, we compare samples from the direct method to various conditional diffusion methods (TMPD, \(\Pi\)GDM, DPM), by plotting a sample estimate of the \(L^2\) Wasserstein distance between the sample and the target Gaussian measures (Givens & Shortt, 1984) by using the analytical mean and covariance of the target distribution and empirical estimate of the mean and covariance of the sample distribution. To generate \(p_0(x_0)\), we use an equally spaced grid of vectors \(u_i\in[-5.0,5.0]^2\) for \(i\in1,2,\ldots,32^2\), pick a Matern 5/2 kernel for the covariance function \(k(u_i,u_j)=\left(1+\sqrt{5}|u_i-u_j|+\frac{5}{3}|u_i-u_j|^2\right)\exp\left(-\sqrt{3}|u_i-u_j|\right)\), which defines the prior \(p_0(x_0)=\mathcal{N}(m_0,C_0)\), covariance \(C_0\in\mathbb{R}^{1024\times1024}\) where \(C_{0ij}=k(u_i,u_j)\) and we define the mean as a zero vector \(m_0=0\in\mathbb{R}^{1024}\). To compute analytically the distribution of \(p_0(x_0|y)\), we sample \(y=Hx_0+z,z\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma_y^2I_{d_y})\) and use the standard Gaussian formula to calculate the mean and covariance of \(x_0|y\) which in this case are a complete description of \(p_0(x_0|y)\). The \(L^2\) Wasserstein estimate is plotted over an increasing sample size \(N\in[9,1500]\) and for \(\sigma_y=0.1\) in Figure 1.  
**中文：** 当数据分布 \(p_0(x_0)\) 为（多元）高斯分布时，反向 SDE 是线性 SDE，因此可以显式计算扩散采样目标后验所需的全部量。此外，也可以通过 Cholesky 分解等直接或隐式方法从目标后验采样。在这个简单示例中，作者将直接方法得到的样本与多种条件扩散方法（TMPD、\(\Pi\)GDM、DPM）的样本进行比较：利用目标高斯测度的解析均值与协方差，以及样本分布均值与协方差的经验估计，绘制样本与目标高斯测度之间的 \(L^2\) Wasserstein 距离估计（Givens & Shortt, 1984）。生成 \(p_0(x_0)\) 时，使用定义在 \([-5.0,5.0]^2\) 上、含 \(32^2\) 个等间距向量 \(u_i\)（\(i=1,2,\ldots,32^2\)）的网格，并选用 Matérn 5/2 核作为协方差函数：\(k(u_i,u_j)=\left(1+\sqrt{5}|u_i-u_j|+\frac{5}{3}|u_i-u_j|^2\right)\exp\left(-\sqrt{3}|u_i-u_j|\right)\)。由此定义先验 \(p_0(x_0)=\mathcal{N}(m_0,C_0)\)，其协方差 \(C_0\in\mathbb{R}^{1024\times1024}\)，且 \(C_{0ij}=k(u_i,u_j)\)；均值向量取零，即 \(m_0=0\in\mathbb{R}^{1024}\)。为解析计算 \(p_0(x_0|y)\)，先采样 \(y=Hx_0+z,\ z\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma_y^2I_{d_y})\)，再使用标准高斯公式计算 \(x_0|y\) 的均值和协方差；二者在此完整刻画了 \(p_0(x_0|y)\)。作者在样本量 \(N\in[9,1500]\) 递增时绘制 \(L^2\) Wasserstein 距离估计，噪声标准差取 \(\sigma_y=0.1\)，结果见图1。

### S366 — paragraph
**Source:** p.26 S366
**Original:** We provide an illustration of the mean and uncertainty captured by the diffusion samples using 1500 samples of each diffusion model to produce a Monte-Carlo estimate of the mean and diagonal variance vector, and compare these to the exact mean and diagonal variance.  
**中文：** 作者使用每种扩散模型各 1500 个样本估计均值向量和对角方差向量，以此展示扩散样本所刻画的均值与不确定性，并将其与精确均值和对角方差进行比较。

<span id="S367"></span>
### S367 — paragraph
**Source:** p.26 S367
**Original:** Below, we provide a calculation comparing the exact diffusion posterior for the linear diffusion posterior SDE to the approximations used in \(\Pi\)GDM (Song et al., 2023) and TMPD. Let us assume that the target distribution is known, \(p_0(x_0)=\mathcal{N}(m_0,C_0)\). Then, via Bayes’ rule,  
**中文：** 下面给出一项计算，将线性扩散后验 SDE 的精确扩散后验与 \(\Pi\)GDM（Song et al., 2023）和 TMPD 所用的近似进行比较。假设目标分布已知，\(p_0(x_0)=\mathcal{N}(m_0,C_0)\)。由贝叶斯公式，

<span id="S368"></span>
### S368 — equation
**Source:** p.26 S368
**Original:** \[
\begin{aligned}
\log p_{0|t}(x_0|x_t)
&=\log p_{t|0}(x_t|x_0)+\log p_0(x_0)+\text{constant}\\
&=-\frac12(x_t-\sqrt{\alpha_t}x_0)^\top(v_tI_{d_x})^{-1}(x_t-\sqrt{\alpha_t}x_0)\\
&\quad-\frac12(x_0-m_0)^\top C_0^{-1}(x_0-m_0)+\text{constant}\\
&=-\frac12(\sqrt{\alpha_t}x_0)^\top(v_tI_{d_x})^{-1}(\sqrt{\alpha_t}x_0)
+x_t^\top(v_tI_{d_x})^{-1}(\sqrt{\alpha_t}x_0)\\
&\quad-\frac12x_0^\top C_0^{-1}x_0+m_0^\top C_0^{-1}x_0+\text{constant}\\
&=-\frac12(x_0-m_t)^\top\Sigma_t^{-1}(x_0-m_t).
\end{aligned}
\]  
**中文：** \[
\begin{aligned}
\log p_{0|t}(x_0|x_t)
&=\log p_{t|0}(x_t|x_0)+\log p_0(x_0)+\text{常数项}\\
&=-\frac12(x_t-\sqrt{\alpha_t}x_0)^\top(v_tI_{d_x})^{-1}(x_t-\sqrt{\alpha_t}x_0)\\
&\quad-\frac12(x_0-m_0)^\top C_0^{-1}(x_0-m_0)+\text{常数项}\\
&=-\frac12(\sqrt{\alpha_t}x_0)^\top(v_tI_{d_x})^{-1}(\sqrt{\alpha_t}x_0)
+x_t^\top(v_tI_{d_x})^{-1}(\sqrt{\alpha_t}x_0)\\
&\quad-\frac12x_0^\top C_0^{-1}x_0+m_0^\top C_0^{-1}x_0+\text{常数项}\\
&=-\frac12(x_0-m_t)^\top\Sigma_t^{-1}(x_0-m_t).
\end{aligned}
\]

&\qquad\quad\cdot\left(y-H\Sigma_t\left(\frac{\sqrt{\alpha_t}}{v_t}x_t+C_0^{-1}m_0\right)\right).\tag{48}

\]

\]

<span id="S395"></span>
### S395 — equation
**Source:** p.27 S395
**Original:** \(\nabla_{x_t}m_{0|t}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_t}}I_{d_x}-\frac{v_t}{\sqrt{\alpha_t}}(\alpha_tC_0+v_tI_{d_x})^{-1}\approx\frac{\sqrt{\alpha_t}}{v_t}\Sigma_t.\)  
**中文：** \(\nabla_{x_t}m_{0|t}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_t}}I_{d_x}-\frac{v_t}{\sqrt{\alpha_t}}(\alpha_tC_0+v_tI_{d_x})^{-1}\approx\frac{\sqrt{\alpha_t}}{v_t}\Sigma_t.\)

<span id="S396"></span>
### S396 — paragraph
**Source:** p.27 S396
**Original:** Note that since,  
**中文：** 注意到

<span id="S397"></span>
### S397 — equation
**Source:** p.27 S397
**Original:** \[
\begin{aligned}
\frac{\sqrt{\alpha_t}}{v_t}\Sigma_t
&=\frac{\sqrt{\alpha_t}}{v_t}\left(C_0^{-1}+\left(\frac{v_t}{\alpha_t}I_{d_x}\right)^{-1}\right)^{-1},\tag{58}\\
&=\frac{1}{v_t\sqrt{\alpha_t}}\left((\alpha_tC_0)^{-1}+(v_tI_{d_x})^{-1}\right)^{-1},\tag{59}\\
&=\frac{1}{v_t\sqrt{\alpha_t}}\left(v_tI-v_t^2(\alpha_tC_0+v_tI_{d_x})^{-1}\right)\quad\text{Woodbury identity},\tag{60}\\
&=\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_t}}\left(I-v_t(\alpha_tC_0+v_tI_{d_x})^{-1}\right),\tag{61}\\
&=\nabla_{x_t}m_{0|t},\tag{62}
\end{aligned}
\]  
**中文：** \[
\begin{aligned}
\frac{\sqrt{\alpha_t}}{v_t}\Sigma_t
&=\frac{\sqrt{\alpha_t}}{v_t}\left(C_0^{-1}+\left(\frac{v_t}{\alpha_t}I_{d_x}\right)^{-1}\right)^{-1},\tag{58}\\
&=\frac{1}{v_t\sqrt{\alpha_t}}\left((\alpha_tC_0)^{-1}+(v_tI_{d_x})^{-1}\right)^{-1},\tag{59}\\
&=\frac{1}{v_t\sqrt{\alpha_t}}\left(v_tI-v_t^2(\alpha_tC_0+v_tI_{d_x})^{-1}\right)\quad\text{Woodbury 恒等式},\tag{60}\\
&=\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_t}}\left(I-v_t(\alpha_tC_0+v_tI_{d_x})^{-1}\right),\tag{61}\\
&=\nabla_{x_t}m_{0|t},\tag{62}
\end{aligned}
\]

<span id="S398"></span>
### S398 — paragraph
**Source:** p.27 S398
**Original:** this is exact. The other approximation \(\Pi\)GDM is making is \(r_t^2I\approx\Sigma_t\), and note that this approximation is accurate with \(r_t^2=v_t\) as \(t\to0\) but inaccurate as \(t\to1\). But the exact linear SDE can be recovered by instead using TMPD,  
**中文：** 这一关系是精确的。\(\Pi\)GDM 作出的另一项近似是 \(r_t^2I\approx\Sigma_t\)。当 \(t\to0\) 且 \(r_t^2=v_t\) 时，该近似较准确；但当 \(t\to1\) 时则不准确。改用 TMPD 可以恢复精确的线性 SDE：

<span id="S399"></span>
### S399 — equation
**Source:** p.27 S399
**Original:** \[
\begin{aligned}
\nabla_{x_t}\log p_{t|y}(x_t|y)&=\nabla_{x_t}\log p_t(x_t),\tag{63}\\
&\quad+\nabla_{x_t}m_{0|t}H^\top\left(H\frac{v_t}{\sqrt{\alpha_t}}\nabla_{x_t}m_{0|t}H^\top+\sigma_y^2I_{d_y}\right)^{-1}(y-Hm_{0|t})\tag{64}\\
&=\nabla_{x_t}\log p_t(x_t)\tag{65}\\
&\quad+\frac{\sqrt{\alpha_t}}{v_t}\Sigma_tH^\top(H\Sigma_tH^\top+\sigma_y^2I_{d_y})^{-1}
\left(y-\frac{\sqrt{\alpha_t}}{v_t}H\Sigma_tx_t\right).\tag{66}
\end{aligned}
\]  
**中文：** \[
\begin{aligned}
\nabla_{x_t}\log p_{t|y}(x_t|y)&=\nabla_{x_t}\log p_t(x_t),\tag{63}\\
&\quad+\nabla_{x_t}m_{0|t}H^\top\left(H\frac{v_t}{\sqrt{\alpha_t}}\nabla_{x_t}m_{0|t}H^\top+\sigma_y^2I_{d_y}\right)^{-1}(y-Hm_{0|t})\tag{64}\\
&=\nabla_{x_t}\log p_t(x_t)\tag{65}\\
&\quad+\frac{\sqrt{\alpha_t}}{v_t}\Sigma_tH^\top(H\Sigma_tH^\top+\sigma_y^2I_{d_y})^{-1}
\left(y-\frac{\sqrt{\alpha_t}}{v_t}H\Sigma_tx_t\right).\tag{66}
\end{aligned}
\]

<span id="S400"></span>
### S400 — heading
**Source:** p.28 S400
**Original:** E.3 GMM  
**中文：** E.3 高斯混合模型（GMM）

<span id="S401"></span>
### S401 — paragraph
**Source:** p.28 S401
**Original:** For a given dimension \(d_x\), we consider \(p_0\) a mixture of 25 Gaussian random variables. The components have mean \(\mu_{i,j}:=(8i,8j,\ldots,8i,8j)\in\mathbb{R}^{d_x}\) for \((i,j)\in\{-2,-1,0,1,2\}^2\) and unit variance. We have set the associated unnormalized weights \(\omega_{i,j}=1.0\). We have set \(\sigma_\delta^2=10^{-4}\).  
**中文：** 给定维度 \(d_x\)，令 \(p_0\) 为 25 个高斯随机变量构成的混合分布。对 \((i,j)\in\{-2,-1,0,1,2\}^2\)，各分量的均值为 \(\mu_{i,j}:=(8i,8j,\ldots,8i,8j)\in\mathbb{R}^{d_x}\)，方差为单位方差。相应的未归一化权重设为 \(\omega_{i,j}=1.0\)，并令 \(\sigma_\delta^2=10^{-4}\)。

<span id="S402"></span>
### S402 — paragraph
**Source:** p.28 S402
**Original:** Note that \(p_t(x_t)=\int p_{t|0}(x_t|x_0)p_0(x_0)\,dx_0\). As \(p_0(x_0)\) is a mixture of Gaussians, \(p_t(x_t)\) is also a mixture of Gaussians with means \(\sqrt{\alpha_t}\mu_{i,j}\) and unitary variances. Therefore, using automatic differentiation libraries, we can calculate \(\nabla_{x_t}\log p_t(x_t)\). We chose \(\beta_{\max}=500.0\) and \(\beta_{\min}=0.1\). We use 1000 timesteps for the time-discretization. For the pair of dimensions and chosen observation noise standard deviation \((d_x,d_y,\sigma_y)\) the measurement model \((y,H)\) is drawn as follows:  
**中文：** 注意，\(p_t(x_t)=\int p_{t|0}(x_t|x_0)p_0(x_0)\,dx_0\)。由于 \(p_0(x_0)\) 是高斯混合分布，\(p_t(x_t)\) 也是高斯混合分布，其均值为 \(\sqrt{\alpha_t}\mu_{i,j}\)，方差为单位方差。因此，可以使用自动微分库计算 \(\nabla_{x_t}\log p_t(x_t)\)。参数取 \(\beta_{\max}=500.0\)、\(\beta_{\min}=0.1\)，时间离散采用 1000 个步。对于给定的维度和观测噪声标准差组合 \((d_x,d_y,\sigma_y)\)，测量模型 \((y,H)\) 按如下方式生成：

<span id="S403"></span>
### S403 — list_item
**Source:** p.28 S403
**Original:** \(\mathbf{H}\): We first draw \(\tilde{\mathbf{H}}\sim\mathcal{N}(0_{d_y\times d_x},I_{d_y\times d_x})\) and compute the SVD decomposition of \(\tilde{\mathbf{H}}=U S V^\top\). Then, we sample for \((i,j)\in\{-2,-1,0,1,2\}^2\), \(s_{i,j}\) according to a uniform in \([0,1]\). Finally, we set \(\mathbf{H}=U\operatorname{diag}(s_{i,j})_{(i,j)\in\{-2,-1,0,1,2\}^2}V^\top\).  
**中文：** \(\mathbf{H}\)：首先采样 \(\tilde{\mathbf{H}}\sim\mathcal{N}(0_{d_y\times d_x},I_{d_y\times d_x})\)，并对 \(\tilde{\mathbf{H}}\) 作奇异值分解（SVD）：\(\tilde{\mathbf{H}}=USV^\top\)。随后，对 \((i,j)\in\{-2,-1,0,1,2\}^2\) 从区间 \([0,1]\) 上的均匀分布采样 \(s_{i,j}\)。最后令 \(\mathbf{H}=U\operatorname{diag}(s_{i,j})_{(i,j)\in\{-2,-1,0,1,2\}^2}V^\top\)。

<span id="S404"></span>
### S404 — list_item
**Source:** p.28 S404
**Original:** \(\mathbf{y}\): We then draw \(x_\ast\sim p_0\) and set \(\mathbf{y}:=\mathbf{H}x_\ast+\mathbf{z}\) where \(\mathbf{z}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma_y^2I_{d_y})\).  
**中文：** \(\mathbf{y}\)：随后采样 \(x_\ast\sim p_0\)，并令 \(\mathbf{y}:=\mathbf{H}x_\ast+\mathbf{z}\)，其中 \(\mathbf{z}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma_y^2I_{d_y})\)。

<span id="S405"></span>
### S405 — paragraph
**Source:** p.28 S405
**Original:** Once we have drawn both \(x_\ast\sim p_0\) and \((\mathbf{y},\mathbf{H},\sigma_y)\), the posterior can be exactly calculated using Bayes formula and gives a mixture of Gaussians with mixture components \(c_{i,j}\) and associated weights \(\tilde{\omega}_{i,j}\),  
**中文：** 在抽取 \(x_\ast\sim p_0\) 以及 \((\mathbf{y},\mathbf{H},\sigma_y)\) 后，可以通过贝叶斯公式精确计算后验；所得后验仍为高斯混合分布，其混合分量为 \(c_{i,j}\)，对应权重为 \(\tilde{\omega}_{i,j}\)：

<span id="S406"></span>
### S406 — equation
**Source:** p.28 S406
**Original:** \[
\begin{aligned}
c_{i,j}&:=\mathcal{N}\left(\Sigma\left(\frac{\mathbf{H}^\top\mathbf{y}}{\sigma_\delta^2}+\mu_{i,j}\right),\Sigma\right),\tag{67}\\
\tilde{\omega}_{i,j}&:=\omega_{i,j}\mathcal{N}\left(\mathbf{y};\mathbf{H}\mu_{i,j},\sigma_\delta^2I_{d_x}+\mathbf{H}\mathbf{H}^\top\right),\tag{68}
\end{aligned}
\]
where \(\Sigma=\left(I_{d_x}+\frac{1}{\sigma_\delta^2}\mathbf{H}^\top\mathbf{H}\right)^{-1}.\)  
**中文：** \[
\begin{aligned}
c_{i,j}&:=\mathcal{N}\left(\Sigma\left(\frac{\mathbf{H}^\top\mathbf{y}}{\sigma_\delta^2}+\mu_{i,j}\right),\Sigma\right),\tag{67}\\
\tilde{\omega}_{i,j}&:=\omega_{i,j}\mathcal{N}\left(\mathbf{y};\mathbf{H}\mu_{i,j},\sigma_\delta^2I_{d_x}+\mathbf{H}\mathbf{H}^\top\right),\tag{68}
\end{aligned}
\]
其中 \(\Sigma=\left(I_{d_x}+\frac{1}{\sigma_\delta^2}\mathbf{H}^\top\mathbf{H}\right)^{-1}。\)

<span id="S407"></span>
### S407 — paragraph
**Source:** p.28 S407
**Original:** Euler-Maruyama solver To compare the posterior distribution estimated by each algorithm with the target posterior distribution, we use \(10^4\) slices for the SW distance and compare 1000 samples of the continuous SDEs defined by the TMPD, DTMPD, Song et al. (2023) and Chung et al. (2022a) approximations obtained using 1000 Euler-Maruyama time-steps with 1000 samples of the true posterior distribution. Table 3 indicates the Central Limit Theorem (CLT) 95% confidence intervals obtained by considering 20 randomly selected measurement models \((\mathbf{H})\) for each setting \((d_x,d_y,\sigma_y)\).  
**中文：** **Euler–Maruyama 求解器。** 为比较各算法估计的后验分布与目标后验分布，作者使用 \(10^4\) 个切片计算切片 Wasserstein（SW）距离；将 TMPD、DTMPD、Song et al. (2023) 和 Chung et al. (2022a) 近似所定义的连续 SDE 以 1000 个 Euler–Maruyama 时间步离散，取各算法 1000 个样本，并与真实后验分布的 1000 个样本比较。表3报告了中心极限定理（central limit theorem, CLT）95% 置信区间；每种设定 \((d_x,d_y,\sigma_y)\) 均考虑 20 个随机选取的测量模型 \((\mathbf{H})\)。

<span id="S408"></span>
### S408 — paragraph
**Source:** p.28 S408
**Original:** DDPM Table 4 compares 1000 samples of TMPD-D, \(\Pi\)GDM-D and DPS-D which are obtained using 1000 denoising steps and is the extended version of Table 1. We follow Cardoso et al. (2023) and compute the sliced Wasserstein distance using Wasserstein-1 distance. Figure 2 shows the first two dimensions of the estimated posterior distributions corresponding to the configurations \((80,1)\) and \((800,1)\) from Table 4 for one of the randomly generated measurement models \((\mathbf{H})\). These illustrations give us insight into the behaviour of the algorithms and their accuracy in estimating the posterior distribution. We observe that TMPD-D (and the Euler-Maruyama method TMPD) is the only method that covers the modes of the posterior distribution. Finally, a direct comparison to Cardoso et al. (2023) using their original experimental setup is shown in Table 5, which shows competitive performance for posterior sampling compared to Sequential Monte-Carlo, an exact sampling method.  
**中文：** **DDPM。** 表4比较 TMPD-D、\(\Pi\)GDM-D 和 DPS-D 各自的 1000 个样本；这些样本由 1000 个去噪步获得，表4是表1的扩展版本。作者遵循 Cardoso et al. (2023)，使用 Wasserstein-1 距离计算切片 Wasserstein 距离。图2展示了表4中配置 \((80,1)\) 和 \((800,1)\) 对应的估计后验分布的前两个维度，所用测量模型 \((\mathbf{H})\) 为随机生成模型之一。这些示例展示了算法的行为及其估计后验分布的准确性。作者观察到，只有 TMPD-D（以及 Euler–Maruyama 方法 TMPD）能够覆盖后验分布的各个众数。最后，表5给出了采用 Cardoso et al. (2023) 原始实验设置的直接比较；与序贯蒙特卡洛（Sequential Monte-Carlo）这一精确采样方法相比，所提方法在后验采样方面表现具有竞争力。

<span id="S410"></span>
### S410 — heading
**Source:** p.29 S410
**Original:** E.4 Inpainting and super-resolution  
**中文：** E.4 图像修复与超分辨率

<span id="S411"></span>
### S411 — paragraph
**Source:** p.29 S411
**Original:** Since the DPS-D method was derived and tuned specifically for VP-SDE, we look at the VP-SDE experiments in Section E.4.1 separately from the VE-SDE experiments in Section E.4.2. In each comparison, we use the same score network for each method and the same sampling or discretization numerical method. All methods are discretized using 1000 denoising steps. For the Markov chain methods we use DDPM and for the SDE methods we use an Euler-Maruyama discretization. In contrast to DPS(-D) ΠGDM(-D), we observe the robustness of our method across both SDEs and inpainting and super-resolution observation maps.  
**中文：** 由于 DPS-D 方法专为 VP-SDE 推导并调参，VP-SDE 实验（第 E.4.1 节）与 VE-SDE 实验（第 E.4.2 节）分开讨论。每组比较中，各方法使用相同的 score 网络，并采用相同的采样或离散化数值方法。所有方法均离散为 1000 个去噪步；马尔可夫链方法采用 DDPM，SDE 方法采用 Euler–Maruyama 离散化。与 DPS(-D) 和 ΠGDM(-D) 相比，作者观察到所提方法在两类 SDE 以及图像修复和超分辨率观测映射下均表现出鲁棒性。

<span id="S415"></span>
### S415 — paragraph
**Source:** p.30 S415
**Original:** Whereas DPS-D requires a hyperparameter search, there are no hyperparameters for DTMPD-D. For ΠGDM-D we use the algorithm and default hyperparameters as described in Song et al. (2023). For DPS-D we use the algorithm in the codebase provided by the authors Chung et al. (2022a) and we use their suggested hyperparameters for this task, such as step-size and using static-thresholding (clipping the denoised image at each step to a range [−1,1]) whereas DTMPD-D does not require any hyperparameter tuning or static-thresholding.  
**中文：** DPS-D 需要搜索超参数，而 DTMPD-D 不含超参数。对于 ΠGDM-D，作者采用 Song et al. (2023) 描述的算法及默认超参数。对于 DPS-D，作者使用 Chung et al. (2022a) 提供的代码库实现，并采用该任务推荐的超参数，例如步长；同时使用静态阈值处理（每一步都将去噪图像裁剪至 [−1,1]）。相比之下，DTMPD-D 不需要调参或静态阈值处理。

<span id="S417"></span>
### S417 — paragraph
**Source:** pp.30–31 S417
**Original:** We found the hyperparameter suggested for the DDIM method for the VP-SDE in Song et al. (2023) \(r_t^2=v_t/(\alpha_t+v_t)\), which is calculated by assuming the data distribution \(p_0(x_0)\) is a standard normal and then calculating the posterior variance, to give unstable solutions for the algorithm given in Song et al. (2023). To make the method stable, we instead plug the ΠGDM posterior score approximation into a DDIM sampler in a similar way to Algorithm 1, which, for the VP-SDE, brings the algorithm ΠGDM-D closer to our method; we are then able to choose \(r_t^2=v_t/(\alpha_t+v_t)\) for both VP DDIM and VP-SDE methods.  
**中文：** 作者发现，Song et al. (2023) 为 VP-SDE 的 DDIM 方法推荐的超参数为 \(r_t^2=v_t/(\alpha_t+v_t)\)；该取值基于如下假设计算：数据分布 \(p_0(x_0)\) 为标准正态分布，且再计算后验方差，从而使 Song et al. (2023) 给出的算法产生不稳定的解。为使该方法稳定，我们改为像算法1那样将 ΠGDM 后验 score 近似代入 DDIM 采样器；对于 VP-SDE，这使 ΠGDM-D 更接近我们的方法。由此，VP DDIM 和 VP-SDE 两种方法均可取 \(r_t^2=v_t/(\alpha_t+v_t)\)。

(2022a) across LPIPS, MSE, PSNR and SSIM, as shown in Fig. 9 for each inverse problem (each line in the Tables 8 and 9).

**Original:** FFHQ 256×256 For VE-SDE, our FFHQ 256×256 experiment compares diffusion methods (DDPM) DTMPD-D to DPS-D and ΠGDM-D, and results are shown in Table 10. Fig 12 and Fig 13 are a visual summary of Table 10, plotting the LPIPS, SSIM and FID metrics against increasing noise. Some uncurated samples that were used to generate Table 10 are shown in Fig 14 and 15. Since the DPS-D and PiGDM algorithms were developed for the VP-SDE, the method is not performant for the VE-SDE, and the ΠGDM algorithm was only performant in low noise settings, whereas DTMPD-D is performant across a wide range of noise levels.  
**中文：** 对于 VE-SDE，FFHQ 256×256 实验将扩散方法（DDPM）DTMPD-D 与 DPS-D、ΠGDM-D 进行比较，结果见表10。图12和图13从视觉上汇总表10，展示 LPIPS、SSIM 和 FID 指标随噪声增大时的变化。用于生成表10的部分未经筛选样本见图14和图15。由于 DPS-D 和 ΠGDM 算法是为 VP-SDE 开发的，这些算法在 VE-SDE 下表现不佳，而 ΠGDM…… 算法只在低噪声设定下表现良好，而 DTMPD-D 在较宽的噪声范围内均保持良好性能。

<span id="F009"></span>
### F009 — figure (first mention on p.36; printed on p.39)
**Source:** p.39 F009; first mentioned p.36
**Original:** Figure 9: DPS scale hyperparameter search across LPIPS, MSE, PSNR and SSIM for CIFAR-10 4× bicubic interpolation super-resolution with σ = 0.05. Plotted are the mean values ± 2 standard deviations over a 128 sample/batch size, calculated over 10 values of ζ′. We chose an optimal value of 0.15 for the DPS scale hyperparameter in this case.  
**中文：** 图9：在 CIFAR-10 4× 双三次插值超分辨率任务（σ = 0.05）中，DPS 尺度超参数在 LPIPS、MSE、PSNR 和 SSIM 上的搜索结果。图中绘出样本数/批大小为128时的均值 ± 2个标准差；统计基于 ζ′ 的10个取值。本例中选取 DPS 尺度超参数最优值 0.15。

![Figure 9: DPS scale hyperparameter search across four metrics](/assets/papers/translations/f69d81ae0d16b949c706baa6/assets/F009-page39-figure9.png)

## 术语表

| English term | 中文译法 |
|---|---|
| diffusion model | 扩散模型 |
| inverse problem | 逆问题 |
| Tweedie formula | Tweedie 公式 |
| posterior score | 后验 score（得分函数） |
| moment projection | 矩投影 |
| variance exploding (VE) | 方差爆炸型（VE） |
| variance preserving (VP) | 方差保持型（VP） |
| score-based generative model | 基于 score 的生成模型 |
| posterior distribution | 后验分布 |
| posterior mean | 后验均值 |
| posterior covariance | 后验协方差 |
| conditional sampling | 条件采样 |
| inpainting | 图像修复 |
| super-resolution | 超分辨率 |
| Gaussian mixture model (GMM) | 高斯混合模型（GMM） |

## 阅读提示

- 正文第7节集中讨论方法局限与未来工作；附录 E.1 补充计算复杂度、Jacobian 近似和共轭梯度替代方案。
- 结果解读应结合对应任务、噪声水平和指标方向；表格数值及不确定度由原表裁图完整保留。
